    -   `counter` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` — the initial value of the counter block, must be 16 bytes long (the AES block size). The rightmost length bits of this block are used for the counter, and the rest is used for the nonce. For example, if length is set to 64, then the first half of counter is the nonce and the second half is used for the counter.  
        `counter`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`計數器區塊的初始值，必須為 16 位元組（ AES區塊大小）。此區塊最右邊的長度位元用於計數器，其餘部分用於隨機數。例如，如果長度設定為 64，則計數器的前半部是隨機數，後半部用於計數器。
    -   `length` is the number of bits in the counter block that are used for the actual counter. The counter must be big enough that it doesn't wrap.  
        `length`是計數器區塊中實際用於計數的位數。計數器必須足夠大，以避免溢出。
-   for `AES-CBC`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`AES-CBC` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `AES-CBC`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`AES-CBC`
    -   `iv` or the initialization vector, is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`, must be 16 bytes, unpredictable, and preferably cryptographically random. However, it need not be secret (for example, it may be transmitted unencrypted along with the ciphertext).  
        `iv`或初始化向量，可以是`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，必須為 16 位元組，不可預測，並且最好是密碼學隨機的。但是，它不必是秘密的（例如，它可以與密文一起以未加密的形式傳輸）。
-   for `AES-GCM`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`AES-GCM` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `AES-GCM`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`AES-GCM`
    -   `iv` or the initialization vector, is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`, must be 16 bytes, and must be unique for every encryption operation carried out with a given key  
        `iv`或初始化向量，可以是`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，必須為 16 位元組，並且對於使用給定金鑰執行的每次加密操作，其值必須是唯一的。
    -   `additionalData` (optional) is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that contains additional data that will not be encrypted but will be authenticated along with the encrypted data. If `additionalData` is specified, then the same data must be specified in the corresponding call to `decrypt()`: if the data given to the `decrypt()` call does not match the original data, the decryption will throw an exception. The bit length of `additionalData` must be smaller than `2^64 - 1`.  
        `additionalData`（可選）是`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`，其中包含不會加密但將與加密資料一起進行身份驗證的附加資料。如果指定了`additionalData`，則在對應的`decrypt()`呼叫中必須指定相同的資料：如果給`decrypt()`呼叫的資料與原始資料不匹配，解密將會拋出例外。 `additionalData`的位长度必须小于`2^64 - 1`。
    -   `tagLength` (optional, default is `128`) - a `number` that determines the size in bits of the authentication tag generated in the encryption operation and used for authentication in the corresponding decryption. Possible values: `32`, `64`, `96`, `104`, `112`, `120`, or `128`. The AES-GCM specification recommends that it should be `96`, `104`, `112`, `120`, or `128`, although `32` or `64` bits may be acceptable in some applications.  
        `tagLength` （可選，預設值為`128` ）—— `number` ，用於確定加密操作中產生的認證標籤的位數， AES-GCM標籤用於相應解密操作中的身份驗證。可選值： `32`, `64`, `96`, `104`, `112`, `120`或`128`規範建議其值為`96`, `104`, `112`, `120`或`128` ，但在某些應用中， `32`或`64`位也是可以接受的。

`key`

a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that contains the key to be used for decryption. If `RSA-OAEP` is used, this is the `privateKey` property of the [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair) object.

包含用於解密的金鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) 。如果使用`RSA-OAEP` ，則這是 [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair)物件的`privateKey`屬性。

`data`

an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that contains the data to be decrypted (also known as ciphertext)

包含待解密資料（也稱為密文）的`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`сrypto.subtle.deriveBits`([`algorithm`](#crypto_derive_bits_alg), [`baseKey`](#crypto_derive_bits_basekey), [`length`](#crypto_derive_bits_length))

Derives an array of bits from a base key. Returns a `Promise` which will be fulfilled with an `ArrayBuffer` that contains the derived bits. Possible values:

從基本鍵派生出一個位元數組。傳回一個`Promise` ，該`ArrayBuffer`將填入包含派生位元的陣列。可能的值：

`algorithm`

is an object that defines the derivation algorithm to use:

是一個定義要使用的推導演算法的物件：

-   for `HKDF`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`HKDF` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `HKDF`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`HKDF`
    -   `hash` is a string with the digest algorithm to use: `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個包含要使用的摘要演算法的字串： `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `salt` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that represents random or pseudo-random value with the same length as the output of the `digest` function. Unlike the input key material passed into `deriveKey()`, salt does not need to be kept secret.  
        `salt`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，表示一個與`digest`函數輸出長度相同的隨機值或偽隨機值。與傳遞給`deriveKey()`輸入金鑰材料不同，鹽值不需要保密。
    -   `info` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that represents application-specific contextual information used to bind the derived key to an application or context, and enables deriving different keys for different contexts while using the same input key material. This property is required but may be an empty buffer.  
        `info`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，它表示用於將派生密鑰綁定到應用程序或上下文的應用程序特定上下文信息，並允許在使用相同輸入密鑰材料的情況下為不同的上下文派生不同的密鑰。此屬性為必填項，但可為空緩衝區。
-   for `PBKDF2`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`PBKDF2` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `PBKDF2`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`PBKDF2`
    -   `hash` is a string with the digest algorithm to use: `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個包含要使用的摘要演算法的字串： `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `salt` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that represents random or pseudo-random value of at least `16` bytes. Unlike the input key material passed into `deriveKey()`, salt does not need to be kept secret.  
        `salt`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，代表至少`16`位元組的隨機或偽隨機值。與傳遞給`deriveKey()`輸入金鑰材料不同，鹽值不需要保密。
    -   `iterations` is a `number` that represents the number of times the hash function will be executed in `deriveKey()`  
        `iterations`是一個`number` ，表示雜湊函數在`deriveKey()`中執行的次數。
-   for `ECDH`, pass the object with the following keys (since [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>)):  
    對於`ECDH` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件（因為 [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>) ）：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDH`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDH`
    -   `public` is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that represents the public key of the other party. The key must be generated using the same curve as the base key.  
        `public`是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) ，代表對方的公鑰。此密鑰必須使用與基礎密鑰相同的曲線產生。
-   for `X25519`, pass the object with the following keys (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)):  
    對於`X25519` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `X25519`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`X25519`
    -   `public` is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that represents the public key of the other party  
        `public`是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) ，代表對方的公鑰

`baseKey`

is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that represents the input to the derivation algorithm - the initial key material for the derivation function: for example, for `PBKDF2` it might be a password, imported as a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) using [`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`](#crypto_subtle_import_key)

是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) ，它代表推導演算法的輸入－推導函數的初始`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`鑰材料：例如，對於`PBKDF2` ，它可能是密碼，以 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) (https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/reference.html#crypto_subtle_import_key)

`length`

is a number representing the number of bits to derive. For browsers compatibility, the number should be a multiple of `8`

是一個表示要導出的位數的數字。為了與瀏覽器相容，該數字應該是`8`的倍數。

`сrypto.subtle.deriveKey`([`algorithm`](#crypto_derive_key_alg), [`baseKey`](#crypto_derive_key_basekey), [`derivedKeyAlgorithm`](#crypto_derive_key_derivedkeyalg), [`extractable`](#crypto_derive_key_extractable), [`keyUsages`](#crypto_derive_key_keyusages))

Derives a secret key from a master key. Possible values:

從主金鑰派生出私鑰。可選值：

`algorithm`

is an object that defines the derivation algorithm to use:

是一個定義要使用的推導演算法的物件：

-   for `HKDF`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`HKDF` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `HKDF`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`HKDF`
    -   `hash` is a string with the digest algorithm to use: `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個包含要使用的摘要演算法的字串： `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `salt` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that represents random or pseudo-random value with the same length as the output of the `digest` function. Unlike the input key material passed into `deriveKey()`, salt does not need to be kept secret.  
        `salt`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，它表示一個隨機值或偽隨機值，其長度與`digest`函數的輸出長度相同。與傳遞給`deriveKey()`輸入金鑰材料不同，鹽值不需要保密。
    -   `info` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that represents application-specific contextual information used to bind the derived key to an application or context, and enables deriving different keys for different contexts while using the same input key material. This property is required but may be an empty buffer.  
        `info`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，它表示特定於應用程式的上下文信息，用於將派生鍵綁定到應用程式或上下文，並允許在使用相同輸入鍵材料的情況下為不同的上下文派生不同的鍵。此屬性為必填項，但可為空緩衝區。
-   for `PBKDF2`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`PBKDF2` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `PBKDF2`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`PBKDF2`
    -   `hash` is a string with the digest algorithm to use: `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個包含要使用的摘要演算法的字串： `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `salt` is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that represents random or pseudo-random value of at least `16` bytes. Unlike the input key material passed into `deriveKey()`, salt does not need to be kept secret.  
        `salt`是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，代表至少`16`位元組的隨機或偽隨機值。與傳遞給`deriveKey()`輸入金鑰材料不同，鹽值不需要保密。
    -   `iterations` is a `number` that represents the number of times the hash function will be executed in `deriveKey()`  
        `iterations`是一個`number` ，表示雜湊函數在`deriveKey()`中執行的次數。
-   for `ECDH`, pass the object with the following keys (since [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>)):  
    對於`ECDH` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件（因為 [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>) ）：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDH`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDH`
    -   `public` is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that represents the public key of the other party. The key must be generated using the same curve as the base key.  
        `public`是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) ，代表對方的公鑰。此密鑰必須使用與基礎密鑰相同的曲線產生。
-   for `X25519`, pass the object with the following keys (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)):  
    對於`X25519` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `X25519`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`X25519`
    -   `public` is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that represents the public key of the other party  
        `public`是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) ，代表對方的公鑰

`baseKey`

is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that represents the input to the derivation algorithm - the initial key material for the derivation function: for example, for `PBKDF2` it might be a password, imported as a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) using [`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`](#crypto_subtle_import_key).

是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) ，表示推導演算法的輸入－推導函數的初始`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`鑰材料：例如，對於`PBKDF2` ，它可能是密碼，作為 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)使用(https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/reference.html#crypto_subtle_import_key)導入。

`derivedKeyAlgorithm`

is an object that defines the algorithm the derived key will be used for:

是一個定義派生鍵將用於的演算法的物件：

-   for `HMAC`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`HMAC` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `HMAC`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`HMAC`
    -   `hash` is a string with the name of the digest function to use: `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個字串，其中包含要使用的摘要函數的名稱： `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `length` (optional) is a `number` that represents the length in bits of the key. If not specified, the length of the key is equal to the block size of the chozen hash function  
        `length` （可選）是一個`number` ，表示金鑰的位數。如果未指定，則密鑰長度等於所選雜湊函數的區塊大小。
-   for `AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`, or `AES-KW` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)), pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`或`AES-KW` （因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ），傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`, or `AES-KW`, depending on the algorithm used  
        `name`是一個字串，應設定為`AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`或`AES-KW` ，取決於所使用的演算法。
    -   `length` is a `number` that represents the length in bits of the key to generate: `128`, `192`, or `256`  
        `length`是一個`number` ，表示要產生的金鑰的位元長度： `128`, `192`或`256`

`extractable`

is a boolean value that indicates whether it will be possible to export the key

是一個布林值，指示是否可以導出該鍵

`keyUsages`

is an `Array` that indicates what can be done with the derived key. The key usages must be allowed by the algorithm set in `derivedKeyAlgorithm`. Possible values:

`Array`指示可以使用派生金鑰執行哪些操作。金鑰用途必須符合`derivedKeyAlgorithm`中設定的演算法。可能的值：

`encrypt`

key for encrypting messages

用於加密訊息的金鑰

`decrypt`

key for decrypting messages

解密訊息的金鑰

`sign`

key for signing messages

用於簽署訊息的密鑰

`verify`

key for verifying signatures

用於驗證簽署的金鑰

`deriveKey`

key for deriving a new key

用於產生新密鑰的密鑰

`deriveBits`

key for deriving bits

用於匯出位元的關鍵

`wrapKey`

key for wrapping a key

用於包裝鑰匙的金鑰

`unwrapKey`

key for unwrapping a key

解包密鑰的密鑰

`сrypto.subtle.digest`([`algorithm`](#crypto_digest_alg), [`data`](#crypto_digest_data))

Generates a digest of the given data. Takes as its arguments an identifier for the digest algorithm to use and the data to digest. Returns a `Promise` which will be fulfilled with the digest. Possible values:

產生給定資料的摘要。它接受兩個參數：摘要演算法的識別碼和待摘要的資料。傳回一個`Promise` ，該物件將被填入摘要。可能的值：

`algorithm`

is a string that defines the hash function to use: `SHA-1` (not for cryptographic applications), `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`

是一個字串，用於定義要使用的雜湊函數： `SHA-1` （不適用於加密應用）、 `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`

`data`

is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that contains the data to be digested

是包含待消化資料的`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`сrypto.subtle.exportKey`([`format`](#crypto_export_key_format), [`key`](#crypto_export_key_keydata))

Exports a key: takes a key as a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object and returns the key in an external, portable format (since [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>)). If the `format` was `jwk`, then the `Promise` fulfills with a JSON object containing the key. Otherwise, the promise fulfills with an `ArrayBuffer` containing the key. Possible values:

導出鍵：接受一個鍵作為 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)對象，並以外部可移植格式（因為 [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>) ）返回該鍵。若`format`為`jwk` ，則`Promise`會傳回一個包含該鍵的JSON物件。否則，承諾會傳回一個包含該鍵的`ArrayBuffer`物件。可能的值：

`format`

a string that describes the data format in which the key should be exported, can be the following:

用於描述鍵值導出資料格式的字串，可以是以下形式：

`raw`

the raw data format

原始資料格式

`pkcs8`

the [PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208) format

[PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208)格式

`spki`

the [SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1) format

[SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1)格式

`jwk`

the [JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) (JWK) format (since [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>))

[JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) ( JWK ) 格式（自 [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>) ）

`key`

the [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that contains the key to be exported

包含待導出金鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)

`сrypto.subtle.generateKey`([`algorithm`](#crypto_generate_key_alg), [`extractable`](#crypto_generate_key_extractable), [`usage`](#crypto_generate_key_usage))

Generates a new key for symmetric algorithms or key pair for public-key algorithms (since [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>)). Returns a `Promise` that fulfills with the generated key as a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) or [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair) object. Possible values:

為對稱演算法產生新金鑰，或為公鑰演算法產生金鑰對（自 [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>)起）。回傳一個滿足 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)或 [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair)物件且使用產生的金鑰的`Promise`物件。可能的值：

`algorithm`

a dictionary object that defines the type of key to generate and provides extra algorithm-specific parameters:

一個字典對象，用於定義要生成的鍵的類型並提供額外的演算法特定參數：

-   for `RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`, or `RSA-OAEP`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`或`RSA-OAEP` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`, or `RSA-OAEP`, depending on the used algorithm  
        `name`是一個字串，應設定為`RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`或`RSA-OAEP` ，取決於所使用的演算法。
    -   `hash` is a string that represents the name of the `digest` function to use, can be `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個字串，表示要使用的`digest`函數的名稱，可以是`SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
-   for `ECDSA`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`ECDSA` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDSA`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDSA`
    -   `namedCurve` is a string that represents the name of the elliptic curve to use, may be `P-256`, `P-384`, or `P-521`  
        `namedCurve`是一個字串，表示要使用的橢圓曲線的名稱，可以是`P-256`, `P-384`或`P-521`
-   for `HMAC`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`HMAC` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `HMAC`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`HMAC`
    -   `hash` is a string that represents the name of the `digest` function to use, can be `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個字串，表示要使用的`digest`函數的名稱，可以是`SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `length` (optional) is a number that represents the length in bits of the key. If omitted, the length of the key is equal to the length of the digest generated by the chosen digest function.  
        `length` （可選）是一個數字，表示金鑰的位數。如果省略，則金鑰長度等於所選摘要函數產生的摘要的長度。
-   for `AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`, or `AES-KW` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)), pass the string identifying the algorithm or an object of the form `{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`, where `ALGORITHM` is the name of the algorithm  
    對於`AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`或`AES-KW`（自[0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)），傳遞標識演算法的字串或`{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`形式的對象，其中`ALGORITHM`是演算法是的名稱
-   for `ECDH`, pass the object with the following keys (since [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>)):  
    對於`ECDH` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件（因為 [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>) ）：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDH`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDH`
    -   `namedCurve` is a string that represents the name of the elliptic curve to use, may be `P-256`, `P-384`, or `P-521`  
        `namedCurve`是一個字串，表示要使用的橢圓曲線的名稱，可以是`P-256`, `P-384`或`P-521`
-   for `Ed25519`, pass the `Ed25519` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "Ed25519" }` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>))  
    對於`Ed25519` ，傳遞`Ed25519`字串或形如`{ "name": "Ed25519" }`的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）
-   for `X25519`, pass the `X25519` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "X25519" }` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>))  
    對於`X25519` ，傳遞`X25519`字串或形如`{ "name": "X25519" }`的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）

`extractable`

boolean value that indicates if it is possible to export the key

布林值，指示是否可以導出密鑰

`usage`

an `array` that indicates possible actions with the key:

`array`表示該金鑰可能執行的操作：

`encrypt`

key for encrypting messages

用於加密訊息的金鑰

`decrypt`

key for decrypting messages

解密訊息的金鑰

`sign`

key for signing messages

用於簽署訊息的密鑰

`verify`

key for verifying signatures

用於驗證簽署的金鑰

`deriveKey`

key for deriving a new key

用於產生新密鑰的密鑰

`deriveBits`

key for deriving bits

用於匯出位元的關鍵

`wrapKey`

key for wrapping a key

用於包裝鑰匙的金鑰

`unwrapKey`

key for unwrapping a key

解包密鑰的密鑰

`сrypto.subtle.importKey`([`format`](#crypto_import_key_format), [`keyData`](#crypto_import_key_keydata), [`algorithm`](#crypto_import_key_alg), [`extractable`](#crypto_import_key_extractable), [`keyUsages`](#crypto_import_key_keyusages))

Imports a key: takes as input a key in an external, portable format and gives a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object. Returns a `Promise` that fulfills with the imported key as a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object. Possible values:

導入鍵：以外部可移植格式的鍵作為輸入，並傳回一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)物件。傳回一個`Promise`對象，該對象滿足導入的鍵作為 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)對象。可能的值：

`format`

a string that describes the data format of the key to import, can be the following:

用於描述要匯入的鍵的資料格式的字串，可以是以下形式：

`raw`

the raw data format

原始資料格式

`pkcs8`

the [PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208) format

[PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208)格式

`spki`

the [SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1) format

[SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1)格式

`jwk`

the [JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) (JWK) format (since [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>))

[JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) ( JWK ) 格式（自 [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>) ）

`keyData`

the `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` object that contains the key in the given format

包含給定格式鍵的`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`對象

`algorithm`

a dictionary object that defines the type of key to import and provides extra algorithm-specific parameters:

一個字典對象，用於定義要導入的鍵的類型並提供額外的演算法特定參數：

-   for `RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`, or `RSA-OAEP`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`或`RSA-OAEP` ，請傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`, or `RSA-OAEP`, depending on the used algorithm  
        `name`是一個字串，應根據所使用的演算法設定為`RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, `RSA-PSS`或`RSA-OAEP` 。
    -   `hash` is a string that represents the name of the `digest` function to use, can be `SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個字串，表示要使用的`digest`函數的名稱，可以是`SHA-1`, `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
-   for `ECDSA`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`ECDSA` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDSA`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDSA`
    -   `namedCurve` is a string that represents the name of the elliptic curve to use, may be `P-256`, `P-384`, or `P-521`  
        `namedCurve`是一個字串，表示要使用的橢圓曲線的名稱，可以是`P-256`, `P-384`或`P-521`
-   for `HMAC`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`HMAC` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `HMAC`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`HMAC`
    -   `hash` is a string that represents the name of the `digest` function to use, can be `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是一個字串，表示要使用的`digest`函數的名稱，可以是`SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
    -   `length` (optional) is a number that represents the length in bits of the key. If omitted, the length of the key is equal to the length of the digest generated by the chosen digest function.  
        `length` （可選）是一個數字，表示金鑰的位數。如果省略，則金鑰長度等於所選摘要函數產生的摘要的長度。
-   for `AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`, or `AES-KW` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)), pass the string identifying the algorithm or an object of the form `{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`, where `ALGORITHM` is the name of the algorithm  
    對於`AES-CTR`, `AES-CBC`, `AES-GCM`或`AES-KW`（自[0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)），傳遞標識演算法的字串或`{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`形式的對象，其中`ALGORITHM`是演算法的名稱
-   for `PBKDF2`, pass the `PBKDF2` string  
    對於`PBKDF2` ，傳遞`PBKDF2`字串
-   for `HKDF`, pass the `HKDF` string  
    對於`HKDF` ，傳遞`HKDF`字串
-   for `ECDH`, pass the object with the following keys (since [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>)):  
    對於`ECDH` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件（因為 [0.9.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.1>) ）：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDH`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDH`
    -   `namedCurve` is a string that represents the name of the elliptic curve to use, may be `P-256`, `P-384`, or `P-521`  
        `namedCurve`是一個字串，表示要使用的橢圓曲線的名稱，可以是`P-256`, `P-384`或`P-521`
-   for `Ed25519`, pass the `Ed25519` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "Ed25519" }` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>))  
    對於`Ed25519` ，傳遞`Ed25519`字串或形如`{ "name": "Ed25519" }`的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）
-   for `X25519`, pass the `X25519` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "X25519" }` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>))  
    對於`X25519` ，傳遞`X25519`字串或形如`{ "name": "X25519" }`的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ​​）

`extractable`

boolean value that indicates if it is possible to export the key

布林值，指示是否可以導出密鑰

`keyUsages`

an `array` that indicates possible actions with the key:

`array`表示該鍵可能執行的操作：

`encrypt`

key for encrypting messages

用於加密訊息的金鑰

`decrypt`

key for decrypting messages

解密訊息的金鑰

`sign`

key for signing messages

用於簽署訊息的密鑰

`verify`

key for verifying signatures

用於驗證簽署的金鑰

`deriveKey`

key for deriving a new key

用於產生新密鑰的密鑰

`deriveBits`

key for deriving bits

用於匯出位元的關鍵

`wrapKey`

key for wrapping a key

用於包裝鑰匙的金鑰

`unwrapKey`

key for unwrapping a key

解包密鑰的密鑰

`сrypto.subtle.sign`([`algorithm`](#crypto_sign_alg), [`key`](#crypto_sign_key), [`data`](#crypto_sign_data))

Returns `signature` as a `Promise` that fulfills with an `ArrayBuffer` containing the signature. Possible values:

返回`signature`作為`Promise` ，該`ArrayBuffer`包含簽名。可能的值：

`algorithm`

is a string or object that specifies the signature algorithm to use and its parameters:

是一個字串或對象，用於指定要使用的簽章演算法及其參數：

-   for `RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, pass the string identifying the algorithm or an object of the form `{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`  
    對於`RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5` ，傳遞標識演算法的字串或形如`{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`的物件。
-   for `RSA-PSS`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`RSA-PSS` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `RSA-PSS`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`RSA-PSS`
    -   `saltLength` is a long `integer` that represents the length of the random salt to use, in bytes  
        `saltLength`是一個長整型`integer` ，表示要使用的隨機鹽的長度（以位元組為單位）。
-   for `ECDSA`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`ECDSA` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDSA`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDSA`
    -   `hash` is an identifier for the digest algorithm to use, can be `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是要使用的摘要演算法的標識符，可以是`SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
-   for `HMAC`, pass the string identifying the algorithm or an object of the form `{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`  
    對於`HMAC` ，傳遞標識演算法的字串或形如`{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`的物件。
-   for `Ed25519`, pass the `Ed25519` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "Ed25519" }` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>))  
    對於`Ed25519` ，傳遞`Ed25519`字串或形如`{ "name": "Ed25519" }`的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）

`key`

is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object that the key to be used for signing. If algorithm identifies a public-key cryptosystem, this is the private key.

是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)對象，其中包含用於簽署的金鑰。如果演算法識別出公鑰密碼系統，則此物件為私鑰。

`data`

is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` object that contains the data to be signed

是一個包含待簽章資料的`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`對象

`сrypto.subtle.verify`([`algorithm`](#crypto_verify_alg), [`key`](#crypto_verify_key), [`signature`](#crypto_verify_signature), [`data`](#crypto_verify_data))

Verifies a digital signature, returns a `Promise` that fulfills with a boolean value: `true` if the signature is valid, otherwise `false`. Possible values:

驗證數位簽名，傳回一個布林值`Promise` ，該布林值符合以下條件：如果簽名有效，則回傳`true`否則`false` 。可能的值：

`algorithm`

is a string or object that specifies the algorithm to use and its parameters:

是一個字串或對象，用於指定要使用的演算法及其參數：

-   for `RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5`, pass the string identifying the algorithm or an object of the form `{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`  
    對於`RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5` ，傳遞標識演算法的字串或形如`{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`的物件。
-   for `RSA-PSS`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`RSA-PSS` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `RSA-PSS`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`RSA-PSS`
    -   `saltLength` is a long `integer` that represents the length of the random salt to use, in bytes  
        `saltLength`是一個長整型`integer` ，表示要使用的隨機鹽的長度（以位元組為單位）。
-   for `ECDSA`, pass the object with the following keys:  
    對於`ECDSA` ，傳遞具有以下鍵的物件：
    -   `name` is a string, should be set to `ECDSA`  
        `name`是一個字串，應該設定為`ECDSA`
    -   `hash` is an identifier for the digest algorithm to use, can be `SHA-256`, `SHA-384`, or `SHA-512`  
        `hash`是摘要演算法的標識符，可以是`SHA-256`, `SHA-384`或`SHA-512`
-   for `HMAC`, pass the string identifying the algorithm or an object of the form `{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`  
    對於`HMAC` ，傳遞標識演算法的字串或形如`{ "name": "ALGORITHM" }`的物件。
-   for `Ed25519`, pass the `Ed25519` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "Ed25519" }` (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>))  
    對於`Ed25519` ，傳遞`Ed25519`字串或形如`{ "name": "Ed25519" }`的物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）

`key`

is a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object that the key to be used for verifying. It is the secret key for a symmetric algorithm and the public key for a public-key system.

是一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)對象，它是用來驗證的金鑰。對於對稱演算法，它是私鑰；對於公鑰系統，它是公鑰。

`signature`

is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that contains the signature to verify

是包含用於驗證的簽章的`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`data`

is an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` object that contains the data whose signature is to be verified

是一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`對象，其中包含待驗證簽署的資料。

`сrypto.subtle.wrapKey`([`format`](#crypto_wrap_key_format), [`key`](#crypto_wrap_key_key), [`wrappingKey`](#crypto_wrap_key_wrappingkey), [`wrapAlgorithm`](#crypto_wrap_key_wrapalg))

Wraps a key: exports a key in an external, portable format, then encrypts the exported key (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)). Returns a `Promise` that fulfills with an `ArrayBuffer` containing the encrypted exported key. Possible values:

封裝機密：將金鑰匯出為外部可移植格式，然後加密匯出的金鑰（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）。傳回一個滿足條件的`Promise` ，其中包含`ArrayBuffer`後的導出金鑰。可能的值：

`format`

a string that describes the data format of the key to export before it is encrypted, can be the following:

用於描述密鑰在加密前的資料格式的字串，可以是以下形式：

`raw`

the raw data format

原始資料格式

`pkcs8`

the [PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208) format

[PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208)格式

`spki`

the [SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1) format

[SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1)格式

`jwk`

the [JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) (JWK) format

[JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) ( JWK ) 格式

`key`

a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that contains the key to be wrapped. The key must have [`CryptoKey.extractable`](#cryptokey_extractable) set to `true`.

包含要包裝的金鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) 。此金鑰必須將 [`CryptoKey.extractable`](#cryptokey_extractable)設定為`true` 。

`wrappingKey`

a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that contains the key used to encrypt the exported key. The key must have the `wrapKey` usage set.

包含用於加密導出金鑰的金鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) 。此金鑰必須設定`wrapKey`用途。

`wrapAlgorithm`

an object that specifies the algorithm to be used to encrypt the exported key, and any extra parameters as required:

一個用於指定加密導出金鑰的演算法以及任何必需的額外參數的物件：

-   for `AES-CBC`, `AES-CTR`, or `AES-GCM`, pass the corresponding algorithm parameters as described in [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)  
    對於`AES-CBC`, `AES-CTR`或`AES-GCM` ，請依照 [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)中的描述傳遞對應的演算法參數。
-   for `AES-KW`, pass the `AES-KW` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "AES-KW" }`  
    對於`AES-KW` ，傳遞`AES-KW`字串或形如`{ "name": "AES-KW" }`的對象
-   for `RSA-OAEP`, pass the corresponding algorithm parameters as described in [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)  
    對於`RSA-OAEP` ，傳遞 [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)中所描述的對應演算法參數

`сrypto.subtle.unwrapKey`([`format`](#crypto_unwrap_key_format), [`wrappedKey`](#crypto_unwrap_key_wrappedkey), [`unwrappingKey`](#crypto_unwrap_key_unwrappingkey), [`unwrapAlgorithm`](#crypto_unwrap_key_unwrapalg), [`unwrappedKeyAlgorithm`](#crypto_unwrap_key_unwrappedkeyalg), [`extractable`](#crypto_unwrap_key_extractable), [`keyUsages`](#crypto_unwrap_key_keyusages))

Unwraps a key: decrypts a wrapped key and creates a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object from the result (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)). Returns a `Promise` that fulfills with the unwrapped key as a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey). Possible values:

解包金鑰：解密已包裝的金鑰，並根據結果建立一個 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)物件（因為 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>) ）。傳回一個滿足 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)的解包金鑰的`Promise`物件。可能的值：

`format`

a string that describes the data format of the key that was wrapped, can be the following:

用來描述被包裝鍵的資料格式的字串可以是以下形式：

`raw`

the raw data format

原始資料格式

`pkcs8`

the [PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208) format

[PKCS #8](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5208)格式

`spki`

the [SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1) format

[SubjectPublicKeyInfo](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5280#section-4.1)格式

`jwk`

the [JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) (JWK) format

[JSON Web Key](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517) ( JWK ) 格式

`wrappedKey`

an `ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView` that contains the wrapped key in the given format

一個`ArrayBuffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` ，其中包含以給定格式包裝的金鑰

`unwrappingKey`

a [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) that contains the key used to decrypt the wrapped key. The key must have the `unwrapKey` usage set.

包含用於解密包裝金鑰的金鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) 。此金鑰必須設定`unwrapKey`用途。

`unwrapAlgorithm`

an object that specifies the algorithm to be used to decrypt the wrapped key, and any extra parameters as required:

一個用於指定解密封裝密鑰的演算法的對象，以及任何必需的額外參數：

-   for `AES-CBC`, `AES-CTR`, or `AES-GCM`, pass the corresponding algorithm parameters as described in [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)  
    對於`AES-CBC`, `AES-CTR`或`AES-GCM` ，請依照 [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)中的描述傳遞對應的演算法參數。
-   for `AES-KW`, pass the `AES-KW` string or an object of the form `{ "name": "AES-KW" }`  
    對於`AES-KW` ，傳遞`AES-KW`字串或形如`{ "name": "AES-KW" }`的對象
-   for `RSA-OAEP`, pass the corresponding algorithm parameters as described in [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)  
    對於`RSA-OAEP` ，傳遞 [`сrypto.subtle.encrypt()`](#crypto_subtle_encrypt)中所描述的對應演算法參數。

`unwrappedKeyAlgorithm`

a dictionary object that defines the type of key to unwrap and provides extra algorithm-specific parameters. The same algorithm descriptions as in [`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`](#crypto_subtle_import_key) apply.

一個字典對象，用於定義要解包的鍵的類型並提供額外的演算法特定參數。與 [`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`](#crypto_subtle_import_key)中相同的演算法描述適用。

`extractable`

boolean value that indicates if it is possible to export the key

布林值，指示是否可以導出密鑰

`keyUsages`

an `array` that indicates possible actions with the key:

`array`表示該金鑰可能執行的操作：

`encrypt`

key for encrypting messages

用於加密訊息的金鑰

`decrypt`

key for decrypting messages

解密訊息的金鑰

`sign`

key for signing messages

用於簽署訊息的密鑰

`verify`

key for verifying signatures

用於驗證簽署的金鑰

`deriveKey`

key for deriving a new key

用於產生新密鑰的密鑰

`deriveBits`

key for deriving bits

用於匯出位元的關鍵

`wrapKey`

key for wrapping a key

用於包裝鑰匙的金鑰

`unwrapKey`

key for unwrapping a key

解包密鑰的密鑰

#### CryptoKey｜加密金鑰

|   |
| --- |
| [`CryptoKey.algorithm`](#cryptokey_alg) |
| [`CryptoKey.extractable`](#cryptokey_extractable) |
| [`CryptoKey.type`](#cryptokey_type) |
| [`CryptoKey.usages`](#cryptokey_usages) |

The `CryptoKey` object represents a cryptographic `key` obtained from one of the `SubtleCrypto` methods: [`сrypto.subtle.generateKey()`](#crypto_subtle_generate_key), [`сrypto.subtle.deriveKey()`](#crypto_subtle_derive_key), [`сrypto.subtle.importKey()`](#crypto_subtle_import_key).

`CryptoKey` `сrypto.subtle.importKey()`表示透過`сrypto.subtle.deriveKey()` `SubtleCrypto`方法之一獲得的加密`key` ：[`сrypto.subtle.generateKey()`](#crypto_subtle_generate_key) , [ (https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/reference.html#crypto_subtle_derive_key) (https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/reference.html#crypto_subtle_import_key) 。

`CryptoKey.algorithm`

returns an object describing the algorithm for which this key can be used and any associated extra parameters (since [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)), read-only

傳回一個對象，該物件描述此鍵可用於的演算法以及任何關聯的額外參數（自 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)起），唯讀

`CryptoKey.extractable`

a boolean value, `true` if the key can be exported (since [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)), read-only

布林值， `true`表示該鍵是否可以導出（因為 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>) ），唯讀

`CryptoKey.type`

a string value that indicates which kind of key is represented by the object, read-only. Possible values:

一個字串值，指示物件所代表的鍵的類型，只讀。可能的值：

`secret`

This key is a secret key for use with a symmetric algorithm.

這是一個用於對稱演算法的金鑰。

`private`

This key is the private half of an asymmetric algorithm's [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair)

此金鑰是非對稱演算法 [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair)的私鑰部分

`public`

This key is the public half of an asymmetric algorithm's [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair).

此金鑰是非對稱演算法的 [`CryptoKeyPair`](#cryptokeypair)的公鑰部分。

`CryptoKey.usages`

An array of strings indicating what this key can be used for (since [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)), read-only. Possible array values:

一個字串數組，指示此鍵的用途（例如 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>) ），唯讀。數組可能的值：

`encrypt`

key for encrypting messages

用於加密訊息的金鑰

`decrypt`

key for decrypting messages

解密訊息的金鑰

`sign`

key for signing messages

用於簽署訊息的密鑰

`verify`

key for verifying signatures

用於驗證簽署的金鑰

`deriveKey`

key for deriving a new key

用於產生新密鑰的密鑰

`deriveBits`

key for deriving bits

用於匯出位元的關鍵

#### CryptoKeyPair｜加密金鑰對

|   |
| --- |
| [`CryptoKeyPair.privateKey`](#cryptokeypair_privatekey) |
| [`CryptoKeyPair.publicKey`](#cryptokeypair_publickey) |

The `CryptoKeyPair` is a dictionary object of the [WebCrypto API](#builtin_crypto) that represents an asymmetric key pair.

`CryptoKeyPair`是 [WebCrypto API](#builtin_crypto)的一個字典對象，表示一個非對稱金鑰對。

`CryptoKeyPair.privateKey`

A [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object representing the private key.

表示私鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)物件。

`CryptoKeyPair.publicKey`

A [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey) object representing the public key.

表示公鑰的 [`CryptoKey`](#cryptokey)物件。

#### njs｜紐澤西州

|   |
| --- |
| [`njs.version`](#njs_version) |
| [`njs.version_number`](#njs_version_number) |
| [`njs.dump()`](#njs_dump) |
| [`njs.memoryStats`](#njs_memory_stats) |
| [`njs.on()`](#njs_on) |

The `njs` object is a global object that represents the current VM instance (since [0.2.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.2.0>)).

`njs`對像是一個全域對象，表示目前的VM實例（因為 [0.2.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.2.0>) ）。

`njs.version`

Returns a string with the current version of njs (for example, “0.7.4”).

傳回包含 njs 目前版本的字串（例如，「 0.7.4 」）。

`njs.version_number`

Returns a number with the current version of njs. For example, “0.7.4” is returned as `0x000704` (since [0.7.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.4>)).

傳回一個包含目前 njs 版本的數字。例如，「 0.7.4 」回傳為`0x000704` （因為 [0.7.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.4>) ）。

``njs.dump(`*value*`)``

` `njs.dump(` *值* `)` `

Returns the pretty-print string representation for a value.

傳回值的美化列印字串表示形式。

`njs.memoryStats`

Object containing memory statistics for current VM instance (since [0.7.8](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.8>)).

包含目前VM實例的記憶體統計資訊的物件（自 [0.7.8](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.8>)以來）。

`size`

amount of memory in bytes njs memory pool claimed from the operating system.

njs 記憶體池從作業系統中認領的記憶體量（以位元組為單位）。

``njs.on(`*event*`, `*callback*`)``

` `njs.on(` *事件* `, ` *回呼* `)` `

Registers a callback for the specified VM event (since [0.5.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.5.2>)). An event may be one of the following strings:

為指定的VM事件註冊回呼（自 [0.5.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.5.2>)起）。事件可以是以下字串之一：

`exit`

is called before the VM is destroyed. The callback is called without arguments.

在VM銷毀之前呼叫。回調函數呼叫時不帶任何參數。

#### process｜過程

|   |
| --- |
| [`process.argv`](#process_argv) |
| [`process.env`](#process_env) |
| [`process.kill()`](#process_kill) |
| [`process.pid`](#process_pid) |
| [`process.ppid`](#process_ppid) |

The `process` object is a global object that provides information about the current process ([0.3.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.3>)).

`process`對像是一個全域對象，提供有關當前進程的資訊（[0.3.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.3>) ）。

`process.argv`

Returns an array that contains the command line arguments passed when the current process was launched.

傳回一個數組，其中包含啟動目前進程時傳遞的命令列參數。

`process.env`

Returns an object containing the user environment.

傳回一個包含使用者環境的物件。

> By default, nginx removes all environment variables inherited from its parent process except the TZ variable. Use the [env](<125 [01.01 文件] 核心功能.md#env>) directive to preserve some of the inherited variables.
>
> 預設情況下，nginx 會移除從其父進程繼承的所有環境變量，但會保留TZ變數。使用 [env](<125 [01.01 文件] 核心功能.md#env>)指令可以保留部分繼承的變數。

``process.kill(`*pid*`, `*number*` | `*string*`)``

` `process.kill(` *pid* `, ` *number* ` | ` *string* `)` `

Sends the signal to the process identified by `pid`. Signal names are numbers or strings such as 'SIGINT' or 'SIGHUP'. See [kill(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/kill.2.html) for more information.

向由`pid`標識的進程發送訊號。訊號名稱為數字或字串，例如' SIGINT '或' SIGHUP '。有關更多信息，請參閱[kill(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/kill.2.html) 。

`process.pid`

Returns the PID of the current process.

返回目前進程的PID 。

`process.ppid`

Returns the PID of the current parent process.

傳回目前父進程的PID 。

#### String｜細繩

By default all strings in njs are Unicode strings. They correspond to ECMAScript strings that contain Unicode characters. Before [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>), byte strings were also supported.

預設情況下，njs 中的所有字串都是 Unicode 字串。它們對應於包含 Unicode 字元的 ECMAScript 字串。在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)之前，也支援位元組字串。

#### Byte strings｜位元組字串

> Since [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>), the support for byte strings and byte string methods were removed. When working with byte sequence, the [Buffer](#buffer) object and `Buffer` properties, such as [`r.requestBuffer`](#r_request_buffer), [`r.rawVariables`](#r_raw_variables), should be used.
>
> 自 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)起，已移除對位元組字串和位元組字串方法的支援。處理位元組序列時，應使用 [Buffer](#buffer)物件和`Buffer`屬性，例如 [`r.requestBuffer`](#r_request_buffer) 、[`r.rawVariables`](#r_raw_variables) 。

Byte strings contain a sequence of bytes and are used to serialize Unicode strings to external data and deserialize from external sources. For example, the [toUTF8()](#string_toutf8) method serializes a Unicode string to a byte string using UTF-8 encoding:

位元組字串包含一系列位元組，用於將 Unicode 字串序列化為外部資料以及從外部來源反序列化。例如，[toUTF8()](#string_toutf8)方法使用UTF -8 編碼將 Unicode 字串序列化為位元組字串：

> ```
> >> '£'.toUTF8().toString('hex')
> 'c2a3'  /* C2 A3 is the UTF-8 representation of 00A3 ('£') code point */
>
> 'c2a3' /* C2 A3 是 00A3 ('£') 代碼點的UTF -8 表示 */
> ```

The [toBytes()](#string_tobytes) method serializes a Unicode string with code points up to 255 into a byte string, otherwise, `null` is returned:

[toBytes()](#string_tobytes)方法將碼點不超過 255 的 Unicode 字串序列化為位元組字串，否則傳回`null` ：

> ```
> >> '£'.toBytes().toString('hex')
> 'a3'  /* a3 is a byte equal to 00A3 ('£') code point  */
>
> 'a3' /* a3 是一個位元組，等於 00A3（'£'）碼點 */
> ```

``String.bytesFrom(`*array*` | `*string*`, `*encoding*`)``

` `String.bytesFrom(` *陣列* ` | ` *字串* `, ` *編碼* `)` `

The method was made obsolete in [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) and was removed in [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>). The `Buffer.from` method should be used instead:

此方法在 [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)中被棄用，並在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)中被移除。應改用`Buffer.from`方法：

> ```
> >> Buffer.from([0x62, 0x75, 0x66, 0x66, 0x65, 0x72]).toString()
> 'buffer'
>
> '緩衝'
> 
> >> Buffer.from('YnVmZmVy', 'base64').toString()
> 'buffer'
>
> '緩衝'
> ```

Before [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>), created a byte string either from an array that contained octets, or from an encoded string ([0.2.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.2.3>)), the encoding could be `hex`, `base64`, and `base64url`.

在 [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)之前，從包含八位元組的陣列或編碼字串建立`base64url`組字串（[0.2.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.2.3>) ），編碼可以是`hex`, `base64`

``String.prototype.fromBytes(`*start*`[, `*end*`])``

` `String.prototype.fromBytes(` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `])` `

the property was made obsolete in [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) and was removed in [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>). Before [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>), returned a new Unicode string from a byte string where each byte was replaced with a corresponding Unicode code point.

此屬性在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)中被棄用，並在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)中被移除。在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)之前，它會從位元組字串傳回新的 Unicode 字串，其中每個位元組都被替換為對應的 Unicode 碼位。

``String.prototype.fromUTF8(`*start*`[, `*end*`])``

` `String.prototype.fromUTF8(` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `])` `

the property was made obsolete in [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) and was removed in [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>). The [`TextDecoder`](#textedeoder) method should be used instead. Before [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>), converted a byte string containing a valid UTF-8 string into a Unicode string, otherwise `null` was returned.

此屬性在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)中被棄用，並在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)中被移除。應改用 [`TextDecoder`](#textedeoder)方法。在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)之前，此方法會將包含有效 -8 字串UTF位元組字串轉換為 Unicode 字串，否則傳回`null` 。

``String.prototype.toBytes(`*start*`[, `*end*`])``

` `String.prototype.toBytes(` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `])` `

the property was made obsolete in [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) and was removed in [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>). Before [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>), serialized a Unicode string to a byte string, returned `null` if a character larger than 255 was found in the string.

此屬性在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)中被棄用，並在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)中被移除。在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)之前，將 Unicode 字串序列化為位元組字串，如果在字串中找到大於 255 的字符，則傳回`null` 。

``String.prototype.toString(`*encoding*`)``

` `String.prototype.toString(` *編碼* `)` `

the property was made obsolete in [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) and was removed in [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>). Before [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>), encoded a string to `hex`, `base64`, or `base64url`:

此屬性在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)中被棄用，並在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)中被移除。在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)之前，字串被編碼為`hex`, `base64`或`base64url` ：

> ```
> >>  'αβγδ'.toString('base64url')
> >>
> >>  'abgd'.toString('base64url')
> 'zrHOss6zzrQ'
> ```

Before version [0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>), only a [byte string](#string_tobytes) could be encoded:

在版本 [0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>)之前，只能對 [位元組字串](#string_tobytes)進行編碼：

> ```
> >>  'αβγδ'.toUTF8().toString('base64url')
> >>
> >>  'abgd'.toUTF8().toString('base64url')
> 'zrHOss6zzrQ'
> ```

``String.prototype.toUTF8(`*start*`[, `*end*`])``

` `String.prototype.toUTF8(` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `])` `

the property was made obsolete in [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) and was removed in [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>). The [`TextEncoder`](#textencoder) method should be used instead. Before [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>), serialized a Unicode string to a byte string using UTF-8 encoding:

此屬性在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)中被棄用，並在 [0.8.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.0>)中被移除。應改用 [`TextEncoder`](#textencoder)方法。在 [0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)之前，使用UTF -8 編碼將 Unicode 字串序列化為位元組字串：

> ```
> >> 'αβγδ'.toUTF8().length
> >>
> >> 'abgd'.toUTF8().length
> 8
> >> 'αβγδ'.length
> >>
> >> 'abgd'.長度
> 4
> ```

#### web API｜網頁API

#### Text Decoder｜文字解碼器

|   |
| --- |
| [`TextDecoder()`](#textdecoder_constructor) |
| [`TextDecoder.prototype.encoding`](#textdecoder_encoding) |
| [`TextDecoder.prototype.fatal`](#textdecoder_fatal) |
| [`TextDecoder.prototype.ignoreBOM`](#textdecoder_ignorebom) |
| [`TextDecoder.prototype.decode()`](#textdecoder_decode) |

The `TextDecoder` produces a stream of code points from a stream of bytes ([0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>)).

`TextDecoder`從位元組流產生程式碼點流（[0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>) ）。

``TextDecoder([[`*encoding*`], `*options*`])``

` `TextDecoder([[` *編碼* `], ` *選項* `])` `

Creates a new `TextDecoder` object for specified `encoding`, currently, only UTF-8 is supported. The `options` is `TextDecoderOptions` dictionary with the property:

為指定的`encoding`創建一個新的`TextDecoder`對象，目前僅支援UTF-8。 `options` 是 `TextDecoderOptions` 字典，具有以下屬性：

`fatal`

boolean flag indicating if [`TextDecoder.decode()`](#textdecoder_decode) must throw the `*TypeError*` exception when a coding error is found, by default is `false`.

布林標誌指示當發現編碼錯誤時，[`TextDecoder.decode()`](#textdecoder_decode)是否必須拋出`*TypeError*`異常，預設值為`false` 。

`TextDecoder.prototype.encoding`

Returns a string with the name of the encoding used by [`TextDecoder()`](#textdecoder), read-only.

傳回一個字串，其中包含 [`TextDecoder()`](#textdecoder)使用的編碼名稱，唯讀。

`TextDecoder.prototype.fatal`

boolean flag, `true` if the error mode is fatal, read-only.

布爾標誌， `true`若錯誤模式為致命，則為唯讀。

`TextDecoder.prototype.ignoreBOM`

boolean flag, `true` if the byte order marker is ignored, read-only.

布林標誌， `true`表示忽略位元組順序標記，唯讀。

``TextDecoder.prototype.decode(`*buffer*`, [`*options*`])``

` `TextDecoder.prototype.decode(` *緩衝區* `, [` *選項* `])` `

Returns a string with the text decoded from the `buffer` by [`TextDecoder()`](#textdecoder). The buffer can be `ArrayBuffer`. The `options` is `TextDecodeOptions` dictionary with the property:

傳回一個字串，其中包含從`buffer`解碼的文本，解碼過程由 [`TextDecoder()`](#textdecoder)完成。緩衝區可以是`ArrayBuffer`是`TextDecodeOptions` `options` ，其屬性為：

`stream`

boolean flag indicating if additional data will follow in subsequent calls to `decode()`: `true` if processing the data in chunks, and `false` for the final chunk or if the data is not chunked. By default is `false`.

布林標誌，指示在後續調用`decode()`: `true`時是否會添加其他數據（如果數據分塊處理），以及在最後一個數據塊或數據未分塊處理時是否會添加其他數據`false` ）。預設值為`false` 。

> ```
> >> (new TextDecoder()).decode(new Uint8Array([206,177,206,178]))
> αβ
>
> ab
> ```

#### Text Encoder｜文字編碼器

|   |
| --- |
| [`TextEncoder()`](#textencoder_constructor) |
| [`TextEncoder.prototype.encode()`](#textencoder_encode) |
| [`TextEncoder.prototype.encodeInto()`](#textencoder_encodeinto) |

The `TextEncoder` object produces a byte stream with UTF-8 encoding from a stream of code points ([0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>)).

`TextEncoder`物件從程式碼點流 ([0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>) ) 產生具有UTF -8 編碼的位元組流。

`TextEncoder()`

Returns a newly constructed `TextEncoder` that will generate a byte stream with UTF-8 encoding.

傳回一個新構造的`TextEncoder` ，它將產生一個使用UTF -8 編碼的位元組流。

``TextEncoder.prototype.encode(`*string*`)``

` `TextEncoder.prototype.encode(` *字串* `)` `

Encodes `string` into a `Uint8Array` with UTF-8 encoded text.

將`string`編碼為`Uint8Array` ，其中UTF為 -8 編碼文字。

``TextEncoder.prototype.encodeInto(`*string*`, `*uint8Array*`)``

` `TextEncoder.prototype.encodeInto(` *字串* `, ` *uint8陣列* `)` `

Encodes a `string` to UTF-8, puts the result into destination `Uint8Array`, and returns a dictionary object that shows the progress of the encoding. The dictionary object contains two members:

將`string`編碼為UTF -8，並將結果放入目標`Uint8Array` ，並傳回一個顯示編碼進度的字典物件。該字典物件包含兩個成員：

`read`

the number of UTF-16 units of code from the source `string` converted to UTF-8

原始碼`string`中的UTF -16 個代碼單元被轉換為UTF -8 個代碼單元。

`written`

the number of bytes modified in the destination `Uint8Array`

目標位置修改的位元組數`Uint8Array`

#### timers｜計時器

|   |
| --- |
| [`clearTimeout()`](#cleartimeout) |
| [`setTimeout()`](#settimeout) |

``clearTimeout(`*timeout*`)``

` `clearTimeout(` *超時* `)` `

Cancels a `timeout` object created by [`setTimeout()`](#settimeout).

取消由 [`setTimeout()`](#settimeout)創建的`timeout`物件。

``setTimeout(`*function*`, `*milliseconds*`[, `*argument1*`, `*argumentN*`])``

` `setTimeout(` *函數* `, ` *毫秒* `[, ` *參數1* `, ` *參數N* `])` `

Calls a `function` after a specified number of `milliseconds`. One or more optional `arguments` can be passed to the specified function. Returns a `timeout` object.

在執行指定數量的`milliseconds`後呼叫`function` 。可以向指定的函數傳遞一個或多個可選的`arguments` 。回傳一個`timeout`物件。

> ```js
> function handler(v)
>
> 函數處理程序(v)
> {
>     // ...
> }
> 
> t = setTimeout(handler, 12);
> 
> // ...
> 
> clearTimeout(t);
>
> 清除超時(t);
> ```

#### Global functions｜全域函數

|   |
| --- |
| [`atob()`](#atob) |
| [`btoa()`](#btoa) |

``atob(`*encodedData*`)``

` `atob(` *編碼資料* `)` `

Decodes a string of data which has been encoded using `Base64` encoding. The `encodedData` parameter is a binary string that contains Base64-encoded data. Returns a string that contains decoded data from `encodedData`.

解碼使用`Base64`編碼的資料字串。 `encodedData` 參數是包含 Base64 編碼資料的二進位字串。傳回一個字串，其中包含來自 `encodedData` 的解碼資料。

The similar [`btoa()`](#btoa) method can be used to encode and transmit data which may otherwise cause communication problems, then transmit it and use the `atob()` method to decode the data again. For example, you can encode, transmit, and decode control characters such as ASCII values `0` through `31`.

類似的 [`btoa()`](#btoa)方法可用於編碼和傳輸可能導致通訊問題的數據，然後再傳輸並使用`atob()`方法再次解碼數據。例如，您可以對控製字元（例如ASCII值`0`到`31`進行編碼、傳輸和解碼。

> ```js
> const encodedData = btoa("text to encode"); // encode a string
>
> const encodedData = btoa("要編碼的文字"); // 對字串進行編碼
> const decodedData = atob(encodedData); // decode the string
>
> const decodedData = atob(encodedData); // 解碼字串
> ```

``btoa(`*stringToEncode*`)``

` `btoa(` *要編碼的字串* `)` `

Creates a Base64-encoded ASCII string from a binary string. The `stringToEncode` parameter is a binary string to encode. Returns an ASCII string containing the Base64 representation of `stringToEncode`.

從二進位字串建立 Base64 編碼的 ASCII 字串。 `stringToEncode`參數是要編碼的二進位字串。傳回 ASCII 字串，其中包含 `stringToEncode` 的 Base64 表示形式。

The method can be used to encode data which may otherwise cause communication problems, transmit it, then use the [`atob()`](#atob) method to decode the data again. For example, you can encode control characters such as ASCII values `0` through `31`.

此方法可用於對可能導致通訊問題的數據進行編碼，傳輸該數據，然後使用 [`atob()`](#atob)方法再次解碼該數據。例如，您可以對控製字元（例如ASCII值`0`到`31`進行編碼。

> ```js
> const encodedData = btoa("text to encode"); // encode a string
>
> const encodedData = btoa("要編碼的文字"); // 對字串進行編碼
> const decodedData = atob(encodedData); // decode the string
>
> const decodedData = atob(encodedData); // 解碼字串
> ```

#### built-in modules｜內建模組
