``Buffer.alloc(`*size*`[, `*fill*`[, `*encoding*`]]))``

` `Buffer.alloc(` *尺寸* `[, ` *填充* `[, ` *編碼* `]]))` `

Allocates a new Buffer of a specified `*size*`. If `*fill*` is not specified, the Buffer will be zero-filled. If `*fill*` is specified, the allocated Buffer will be initialized by calling [`buf.fill(fill)`](#buf_fill). If `*fill*` and `*encoding*` are specified, the allocated Buffer will be initialized by calling [`buf.fill(fill, encoding)`](#buf_fill).

分配一個指定大小的新緩衝區`*size*` 。如果未指定`*fill*` ，則緩衝區將填入零。如果指定了`*fill*` ，則將透過呼叫 [`buf.fill(fill)`](#buf_fill)來初始化分配的緩衝區。如果指定了`*fill*`和`*encoding*` ，則將透過呼叫 [`buf.fill(fill, encoding)`](#buf_fill)來初始化分配的緩衝區。

The `*fill*` parameter may be a `*string*`, `*Buffer*`, `*Uint8Array*`, or `*integer*`.

`*fill*`參數可能是`*string*`, `*Buffer*`, `*Uint8Array*`或`*integer*` 。

``Buffer.allocUnsafe(`*size*`)``

` `Buffer.allocUnsafe(` *尺寸* `)` `

The same as [`Buffer.alloc()`](#buffer_alloc), with the difference that the memory allocated for the buffer is not initialized, the contents of the new buffer is unknown and may contain sensitive data.

與 [`Buffer.alloc()`](#buffer_alloc)相同，差異在於分配給緩衝區的記憶體未初始化，新緩衝區的內容未知，且可能包含敏感資料。

``Buffer.byteLength(`*value*`[, `*encoding*`])``

` `Buffer.byteLength(` *值* `[, ` *編碼* `])` `

Returns the byte length of a specified value, when encoded using `*encoding*`. The value can be a `string`, `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, `DataView`, or `ArrayBuffer`. If the value is a `*string*`, the `encoding` parameter is its encoding, can be `*utf8*`, `*hex*`, `*base64*`, `*base64url*`; by default is `*utf8*`.

傳回使用`*encoding*`編碼的指定值的位元組長度。該值可以是`string`, `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, `DataView`或`ArrayBuffer` 。如果該值為`*string*` ，則`encoding`參數是其編碼，可以是`*utf8*`, `*hex*`, `*base64*`, `*base64url*` ；預設值為`*utf8*` 。

``Buffer.compare(`*buffer1*`, `*buffer2*`)``

` `Buffer.compare(` *緩衝區1* `, ` *緩衝區2* `)` `

Compares `*buffer1*` with `*buffer2*` when sorting arrays of Buffer instances. Returns `0` if `*buffer1*` is the same as `*buffer2*`, `1` if `*buffer2*` should come before `*buffer1*` when sorted, or `-1` if `*buffer2*` should come after `*buffer1*` when sorted.

比較 `*buffer1*` 和 `*buffer2*` 對 Buffer 實例數組進行排序時傳回結果 `0` 如果 `*buffer1*` 與…相同 `*buffer2*`, `1` 如果 `*buffer2*` 應該在…之前 `*buffer1*` 排序後，或 `-1` 如果 `*buffer2*` 應該在…之後 `*buffer1*` 排序後。

``Buffer.concat(`*list*`[, `*totalLength*`])``

` `Buffer.concat(` *列表* `[, ` *總長度* `])` `

Returns a new Buffer which is the result of concatenating all the Buffer instances in the list. If there are no items in the list or the total length is 0, a new zero-length Buffer is returned. If `*totalLength*` is not specified, it is calculated from the Buffer instances in list by adding their lengths. If `*totalLength*` is specified, it is coerced to an unsigned integer. If the combined length of the Buffers in list exceeds `*totalLength*`, the result is truncated to `*totalLength*`.

傳回一個新的 Buffer，它是連線清單中所有 Buffer 實例的結果。如果清單中沒有項目或總長度為 0，則傳回一個新的零長度 Buffer。如果未指定`*totalLength*`，則透過將清單中的 Buffer 實例的長度相加來計算。如果指定了`*totalLength*`，則將其強制為無符號整數。如果清單中 Buffer 的總長度超過`*totalLength*`，則結果將被截斷為`*totalLength*`。

``Buffer.from(`*array*`)``

` `Buffer.from(` *陣列* `)` `

Allocates a new Buffer using an array of bytes in the range `0` – `255`. Array entries outside that range will be truncated.

使用範圍在`0` – `255`內的位元組數組分配一個新的緩衝區。超出該範圍的陣列條目將被截斷。

``Buffer.from(`*arrayBuffer*`, `*byteOffset*`[, `*length*`]])``

` `Buffer.from(` *arrayBuffer* `, ` *byteOffset* `[, ` *length* `]])` `

Creates a view of the `*ArrayBuffer*` without copying the underlying memory. The optional `*byteOffset*` and `*length*` arguments specify a memory range within the `*arrayBuffer*` that will be shared by the Buffer.

創建`*ArrayBuffer*`的視圖，而不複製底層記憶體。可選的`*byteOffset*`和`*length*`參數指定`*arrayBuffer*`內將由緩衝區共享的記憶體範圍。

``Buffer.from(`*buffer*`)``

` `Buffer.from(` *緩衝區* `)` `

Copies the passed buffer data onto a new Buffer instance.

將傳遞的緩衝區資料複製到新的 Buffer 實例上。

``Buffer.from(`*object*`[, `*offsetOrEncoding*`[, `*length*`]])``

` `Buffer.from(` *物件* `[, ` *偏移量或編碼* `[, ` *長度* `]])` `

For objects whose `valueOf()` function returns a value not strictly equal to object, returns `Buffer.from(object.valueOf()`, `offsetOrEncoding`, `length`).

對於`valueOf()`函數傳回值不嚴格等於 object 的對象，傳回`Buffer.from(object.valueOf()`, `offsetOrEncoding`, `length` ）。

``Buffer.from(`*string*`[, `*encoding*`])``

` `Buffer.from(` *字串* `[, ` *編碼* `])` `

Creates a new Buffer with a `*string*`. The `*encoding*` parameter identifies the character encoding to be used when converting a string into bytes. The encoding can be `utf8`, `hex`, `base64`, `base64url`; by default is `utf8`.

建立一個新的緩衝區`*encoding*`其值為`*string*`參數指定將字串轉換為位元組時使用的字元編碼。編碼可以是`utf8`, `hex`, `base64`, `base64url` ；預設值為`utf8` 。

``Buffer.isBuffer(`*object*`)``

` `Buffer.isBuffer(` *物件* `)` `

A boolean value, returns `true` if `*object*` is a Buffer.

布林值，如果`*object*`是一個緩衝區，則回傳`true` 。

``Buffer.isEncoding(`*encoding*`)``

` `Buffer.isEncoding(` *編碼* `)` `

A boolean value, returns `true` if encoding is the name of a supported character encoding.

布林值，如果 encoding 是受支援的字元編碼的名稱，則傳回`true` 。

``buffer[`*index*`]``

` `buffer[` *索引* `]` `

The index operator that can be used to get and set the octet at position `index` in `buffer`. The values refer to individual bytes, so the legal value range is between 0 and 255 (decimal).

索引運算子可用於取得和設定`buffer`中位置`index`的八位元組。這些值指的是單一位元組，因此合法值範圍為 0 到 255（十進位）。

`buf.buffer`

The underlying `ArrayBuffer` object based on which this Buffer object is created.

建立此 Buffer 物件所依據的底層`ArrayBuffer`物件。

`buf.byteOffset`

An integer, specifying the `byteOffset` of the Buffers underlying `ArrayBuffer` object.

一個整數，指定`ArrayBuffer`物件底層緩衝區的`byteOffset` 。

``buf.compare(`*target*`[, `*targetStart*`[, `*targetEnd*`[, `*sourceStart*`[, `*sourceEnd*`]]]])``

``buf.compare(`*目標*`[, `*目標開始*`[, `*目標結束*`[, `*sourceStart*`[, `*源結束*`]]]])``

Compares buffer with `*target*` and returns a number indicating whether buffer comes before, after, or is the same as `*target*` in sort order. Comparison is based on the actual sequence of bytes in each Buffer. The `targetStart` is an integer specifying the offset within `*target*` at which to begin comparison, by default is 0. The `targetEnd` is an integer specifying the offset within `*target*` at which to end comparison, by default is `target.length`. The `sourceStart` is an integer specifying the offset within buffer at which to begin comparison, by default is 0. The `sourceEnd` is an integer specifying the offset within buffer at which to end comparison (not inclusive), by default is `buf.length`.

將 buffer 與 `*target*` 進行比較，並傳回一個數字，指示 buffer 按排序順序是否位於 `*target*` 之前、之後或相同。比較基於每個緩衝區中的實際位元組順序。 `targetStart` 是一個整數，指定 `*target*` 內開始比較的偏移量，預設為 0。 `targetEnd` 是一個整數，指定 `*target*` 內結束比較的偏移量，預設為 `target.length`。 `sourceStart` 是一個整數，指定緩衝區內開始比較的偏移量，預設為 0。 `sourceEnd` 是一個整數，指定緩衝區內結束比較的偏移量（不包括在內），預設為 `buf.length`。

``buf.copy(`*target*`[, `*targetStart*`[, `*sourceStart*`[, `*sourceEnd*`]]])``

` `buf.copy(` *目標* `[, ` *目標起始點* `[, ` *源起始點* `[, ` *源結束點* `]]])` `

Copies data from a region of buffer to a region in `*target*`, even if the target memory region overlaps with buffer. The `target` parameter is a `*Buffer*` or `*Uint8Array*` to copy into.

將資料從緩衝區的某個區域複製到`*target*`中的區域，即使目標記憶體區域與緩衝區重疊。 `target` 參數是要複製到的 `*Buffer*` 或 `*Uint8Array*`。

The `targetStart` is an integer specifying the offset within target at which to begin writing, by default is 0. The `sourceStart` is an integer specifying the offset within buffer from which to begin copying, by default is 0. The `sourceEnd` is an integer specifying the offset within buffer at which to stop copying (not inclusive) by default is `*buf.length*`.

`targetStart` 是一個整數，指定目標內開始寫入的偏移量，預設為 0。 `sourceStart` 是一個整數，指定緩衝區內開始複製的偏移量，預設為 0。 `sourceEnd` 是一個整數，指定緩衝區內停止複製（不包括）的偏移量，預設為 `*buf.length*`。

``buf.equals(`*otherBuffer*`)``

` `buf.equals(` *其他緩衝區* `)` `

A boolean value, returns `true` if both Buffer and `*otherBuffer*` have exactly the same bytes.

布林值，如果 Buffer 和`*otherBuffer*`具有完全相同的位元組，則傳回`true` 。

``buf.fill(`*value*`[, `*offset*`[, `*end*`]][, `*encoding*`])``

` `buf.fill(` *值* `[, ` *偏移量* `[, ` *結束* `]][, ` *編碼* `])` `

Fills the Buffer with the specified `*value*`. If the `*offset*` and `*end*` are not specified, the entire Buffer will be filled. The `*value*` is coerced to `*uint32*` if it is not a `string`, `Buffer`, or `integer`. If the resulting integer is greater than 255, the Buffer will be filled with `*value*` and 255.

使用指定的`*value*`填滿緩衝區。如果未指定`*offset*`和`*end*` ，則填入整個緩衝區。如果`*value*`不是`string`, `Buffer`或`integer` ，則將其強制轉換為`*uint32*` 。如果轉換後的整數大於 255，則緩衝區將填入`*value*`和 255。

``buf.includes(`*value*`[, `*byteOffset*`][, `*encoding*`])``

` `buf.includes(` *值* `[, ` *位元組偏移量* `][, ` *編碼* `])` `

Equivalent to [`buf.indexOf()`](#buf_indexof) `!== -1`, returns `true` if the `*value*` was found in Buffer.

等價於 [ `buf.indexOf()` ](#buf_indexof) `!== -1` ，如果在緩衝區中找到`*value*` ，則回傳`true` 。

``buf.indexOf(`*value*`[, `*byteOffset*`][, `*encoding*`])``

` `buf.indexOf(` *值* `[, ` *位元組偏移量* `][, ` *編碼* `])` `

Returns an integer which is the index of the first occurrence of `*value*` in Buffer, or `*-1*` if Buffer does not contain value. The `*value*` can be a `string` with specified `*encoding*` (by default `*utf8*`), `Buffer`, `Unit8Array`, or a number between 0 and 255.

傳回一個整數，該整數是緩衝區中首次出現`*value*`的索引；如果緩衝區`*utf8*`沒有值，則傳回`*-1*`可以是指定`string` `*encoding*` `*value*`可以是`Buffer`, `Unit8Array` ，或介於0和255之間的數字。

``buf.lastIndexOf(`*value*`[, `*byteOffset*`][, `*encoding*`])``

` `buf.lastIndexOf(` *值* `[, ` *位元組偏移量* `][, ` *編碼* `])` `

The same as [`buf.indexOf()`](#buf_indexof), except the last occurrence of the `*value*` is found instead of the first occurrence. The `*value*` can be a string, Buffer, or integer between 1 and 255. If the `*value*` is an empty string or empty Buffer, `byteOffset` will be returned.

與 [`buf.indexOf()`](#buf_indexof) 相同，只是找到最後一次出現的 `*value*`，而不是第一次出現。 `*value*` 可以是字串、Buffer 或 1 到 255 之間的整數。如果 `*value*` 是空字串或空 Buffer，則傳回 `byteOffset`。

`buf.length`

Returns the number of bytes in Buffer.

傳回緩衝區中的位元組數。

``buf.readIntBE(`*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.readIntBE(` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Reads the `*byteLength*` from `buf` at the specified `*offset*` and interprets the result as a big-endian, two's complement signed value supporting up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

在指定的 `*offset*` 處從 `buf` 讀取 `*byteLength*` 並將結果解釋為大端序、二進制補碼有符號值，支援高達 48 位元精確度。 `*byteLength*` 參數是 1 到 6 之間的整數，指定要讀取的位元組數。

The similar methods are also supported: `buf.readInt8([offset])`, `buf.readInt16BE([offset])`, `buf.readInt32BE([offset])`.

也支持類似的方法： `buf.readInt8([offset])`, `buf.readInt16BE([offset])`, `buf.readInt32BE([offset])` 。

``buf.readIntLE(`*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.readIntLE(` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Reads the `*byteLength*` from `buf` at the specified `*offset*` and interprets the result as a little-endian, two's complement signed value supporting up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

在指定的 `*offset*` 處從 `buf` 讀取`*byteLength*`，並將結果解釋為小尾數、二進制補碼有符號值，支援高達 48 位元精度。 `*byteLength*` 參數是 1 到 6 之間的整數，指定要讀取的位元組數。

The similar methods are also supported: `buf.readInt8([offset])`, `buf.readInt16LE([offset])`, `buf.readInt32LE([offset])`.

也支持類似的方法： `buf.readInt8([offset])`, `buf.readInt16LE([offset])`, `buf.readInt32LE([offset])` 。

``buf.readUIntBE(`*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.readUIntBE(` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Reads the `*byteLength*` from `buf` at the specified `*offset*` and interprets the result as a big-endian integer supporting up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

在指定的 `*offset*` 處從 `buf` 讀取 `*byteLength*` 並將結果解釋為支援高達 48 位元精確度的大端整數。 `*byteLength*` 參數是 1 到 6 之間的整數，指定要讀取的位元組數。

The similar methods are also supported: `buf.readUInt8([offset])`, `buf.readUInt16BE([offset])`, `buf.readUInt32BE([offset])`.

也支持類似的方法： `buf.readUInt8([offset])`, `buf.readUInt16BE([offset])`, `buf.readUInt32BE([offset])` 。

``buf.readUIntLE(`*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.readUIntLE(` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Reads the `*byteLength*` from `buf` at the specified `*offset*` and interprets the result as a little-endian integer supporting up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

在指定的 `*offset*` 處從 `buf` 讀取 `*byteLength*` 並將結果解釋為支援高達 48 位元精確度的小端整數。 `*byteLength*` 參數是 1 到 6 之間的整數，指定要讀取的位元組數。

The similar methods are also supported: `buf.readUInt8([offset])`, `buf.readUInt16LE([offset])`, `buf.readUInt32LE([offset])`.

也支持類似的方法： `buf.readUInt8([offset])`, `buf.readUInt16LE([offset])`, `buf.readUInt32LE([offset])` 。

`buf.readDoubleBE`(\[`*offset*`\])

Reads a 64-bit, big-endian double from `buf` at the specified `*offset*`.

從`buf`讀取指定`*offset*`處的 64 位元大端雙精確度浮點數。

`buf.readDoubleLE`(\[`*offset*`\])

Reads a 64-bit, little-endian double from `buf` at the specified `*offset*`.

從`buf`讀取指定`*offset*`處的 64 位元小端雙精確度浮點數。

`buf.readFloatBE`(\[`*offset*`\])

Reads a 32-bit, big-endian float from `buf` at the specified `*offset*`.

從`buf`讀取指定`*offset*`處的 32 位元大端浮點數。

`buf.readFloatLE`(\[`*offset*`\])

Reads a 32-bit, little-endian float from `buf` at the specified `*offset*`.

從`buf`讀取指定`*offset*`處的 32 位元小端浮點數。

``buf.subarray([`*start*`[, `*end*`]])``

` `buf.subarray([` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `]])` `

Returns a new `buf` that references the same memory as the original, but offset and cropped by `*start*` and `*end*`. If `*end*` is greater than [`buf.length`](#buf_length), the same result as that of end equal to [`buf.length`](#buf_length) is returned.

返回新的 `buf`，它引用與原始內存相同的內存，但由 `*start*` 和 `*end*` 進行偏移和裁剪。如果`*end*`大於[`buf.length`](#buf_length)，則傳回與[`buf.length`](#buf_length)相同的結果。

``buf.slice([`*start*`[, `*end*`]])``

` `buf.slice([` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `]])` `

Returns a new `buf` that references the same memory as the original, but offset and cropped by the `*start*` and `*end*` values. The method is not compatible with the `Uint8Array.prototype.slice()`, which is a superclass of Buffer. To copy the slice, use `Uint8Array.prototype.slice()`.

傳回一個新的`buf` ，它引用與原始物件相同的內存，但偏移量和裁剪量分別為`*start*`和`*end*`的值。此方法與`Uint8Array.prototype.slice()`不相容，後者是 Buffer 的超類別。若要複製切片，請使用`Uint8Array.prototype.slice()` 。

`buf.swap16`()

Interprets `buf` as an array of unsigned 16-bit numbers and swaps the byte order in-place. Throws an error if [`buf.length`](#buf_length) is not a multiple of 2.

將`buf`解釋為一個 16 位元無符號數數組，並就地交換位元組順序。如果 [`buf.length`](#buf_length)不是 2 的倍數，則拋出錯誤。

`buf.swap32`()

Interprets `buf` as an array of unsigned 32-bit numbers and swaps the byte order in-place. Throws an error if [`buf.length`](#buf_length) is not a multiple of 4.

將`buf`解釋為無符號 32 位元數字數組，並就地交換位元組順序。如果 [`buf.length`](#buf_length)不是 4 的倍數，則拋出錯誤。

`buf.swap64`()

Interprets `buf` as an array of 64-bit numbers and swaps byte order in-place. Throws an error if [`buf.length`](#buf_length) is not a multiple of 8.

將`buf`解釋為 64 位元數字數組，並就地交換位元組順序。如果 [`buf.length`](#buf_length)不是 8 的倍數，則拋出錯誤。

`buf.toJSON`()

Returns a JSON representation of `buf.` `JSON.stringify()` implicitly calls this function when stringifying a Buffer instance.

傳回JSON表示`buf.` `JSON.stringify()`在字串化 Buffer 實例時隱式呼叫此函數。

``buf.toString([`*encoding*`[, `*start*`[, `*end*`]]])``

` `buf.toString([` *編碼* `[, ` *開始* `[, ` *結束* `]]])` `

Decodes `buf` to a string according to the specified character `*encoding*` which can be `*utf8*`, `*hex*`, `*base64*`, `*base64url*`. The `*start*` and `*end*` parameters may be passed to decode only a subset of Buffer.

將`buf`根據指定的字元`*encoding*`解碼為字串，該字元可以是`*utf8*`, `*hex*`, `*base64*`, `*base64url*` 。可以傳遞`*start*`和`*end*`參數，以便僅解碼 Buffer 的子集。

``buf.write(`*string*`[, `*offset*`[, `*length*`]][, `*encoding*`])``

` `buf.write(` *字串* `[, ` *偏移量* `[, ` *長度* `]][, ` *編碼* `])` `

Writes a `*string*` to `buf` at `*offset*` according to the character `*encoding*`. The `*length*` parameter is the number of bytes to write. If Buffer did not contain enough space to fit the entire string, only part of string will be written, however, partially encoded characters will not be written. The `*encoding*` can be `*utf8*`, `*hex*`, `*base64*`, `*base64url*`.

根據字符`*string*` ，在`*offset*`處寫入`buf`參數是要寫入的位元組數。如果`*length*`空間不足以容納整個字串， `*encoding*`只會寫入部分字串，但部分編碼`*utf8*`, `*hex*`, `*base64*`, `*base64url*` `*encoding*`

``buf.writeIntBE(`*value*`, `*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.writeIntBE(` *值* `, ` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Writes `*byteLength*` bytes of `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as big-endian. Supports up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

將指定位置的`*value*` `*byteLength*`資料寫入`*offset*`到`buf` ，採用大端位元組序。支援最高 48 位元精度`*byteLength*`參數為 1 到 6 之間的整數，用於指定要讀取的位元組數。

The following similar methods are also supported: `buf.writeInt8`, `buf.writeInt16BE`, `buf.writeInt32BE`.

也支援以下類似方法： `buf.writeInt8`, `buf.writeInt16BE`, `buf.writeInt32BE` 。

``buf.writeIntLE(`*value*`, `*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.writeIntLE(` *值* `, ` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Writes `*byteLength*` bytes of `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as little-endian. Supports up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

將 `*value*` 的 `*byteLength*` 位元組以小端方式寫入指定的 `*offset*` 處的 `buf`。支援高達 48 位元的精度。 `*byteLength*` 參數是 1 到 6 之間的整數，指定要讀取的位元組數。

The following similar methods are also supported: `buf.writeInt8`, `buf.writeInt16LE`, `buf.writeInt32LE`.

也支援以下類似方法： `buf.writeInt8`, `buf.writeInt16LE`, `buf.writeInt32LE` 。

``buf.writeUIntBE(`*value*`, `*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.writeUIntBE(` *值* `, ` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Writes `*byteLength*` bytes of `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as big-endian. Supports up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

將 `*value*` 的 `*byteLength*` 位元組以大端方式寫入指定的 `*offset*` 處的 `buf`。支援高達 48 位元的精度。 `*byteLength*` 參數是 1 到 6 之間的整數，指定要讀取的位元組數。

The following similar methods are also supported: `buf.writeUInt8`, `buf.writeUInt16BE`, `buf.writeUInt32BE`.

也支援以下類似方法： `buf.writeUInt8`, `buf.writeUInt16BE`, `buf.writeUInt32BE` 。

``buf.writeUIntLE(`*value*`, `*offset*`, `*byteLength*`)``

` `buf.writeUIntLE(` *值* `, ` *偏移量* `, ` *位元組長度* `)` `

Writes `*byteLength*` bytes of `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as little-endian. Supports up to 48 bits of accuracy. The `*byteLength*` parameter is an integer between 1 and 6 specifying the number of bytes to read.

將指定位置的`*value*` `*offset*`資料以小端序寫入`*byteLength*`到`buf` 。支援最高 48 位元精度`*byteLength*`參數是介於 1 和 6 之間的整數，用於指定要讀取的位元組數。

The following similar methods are also supported: `buf.writeUInt8`, `buf.writeUInt16LE`, `buf.writeUInt32LE`.

也支援以下類似方法： `buf.writeUInt8`, `buf.writeUInt16LE`, `buf.writeUInt32LE` 。

``buf.writeDoubleBE(`*value*`, [`*offset*`])``

` `buf.writeDoubleBE(` *值* `, [` *偏移量* `])` `

Writes the `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as big-endian.

將`*value*`到`buf`以大端序寫入指定的`*offset*` 。

``buf.writeDoubleLE(`*value*`, [`*offset*`])``

` `buf.writeDoubleLE(` *值* `, [` *偏移量* `])` `

Writes the `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as little-endian.

將`*value*`到`buf`以小端序寫入指定的`*offset*` 。

``buf.writeFloatBE(`*value*`, [`*offset*`])``

` `buf.writeFloatBE(` *值* `, [` *偏移量* `])` `

Writes the `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as big-endian.

將`*value*`到`buf`以大端序寫入指定的`*offset*` 。

``buf.writeFloatLE(`*value*`, [`*offset*`])``

` `buf.writeFloatLE(` *值* `, [` *偏移量* `])` `

Writes the `*value*` to `buf` at the specified `*offset*` as little-endian.

將`*value*`到`buf`以小端序寫入指定的`*offset*` 。

#### Crypto｜加密貨幣

|   |
| --- |
| [`crypto.createHash()`](#crypto_createhash) |
| [`crypto.createHmac()`](#crypto_createhmac) |

> Since [0.7.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.0>), extended crypto API is available as a global [crypto](#builtin_crypto) object.
>
> 由於 [0.7.0](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.0>) ，擴展加密API可作為全域 [crypto](#builtin_crypto)物件使用。

The Crypto module provides cryptographic functionality support. The Crypto module object is imported using `import crypto from 'crypto'`.

Crypto 模組提供加密功能支援。 Crypto 模組物件使用`import crypto from 'crypto'`導入。

> Since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>), the Crypto module requires OpenSSL and uses OpenSSL EVP for hashing, making any digest algorithm supported by the linked OpenSSL library available to JavaScript code.
>
> 自 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)起，加密模組需要 OpenSSL，並使用 OpenSSL EVP進行哈希，使得連結的 OpenSSL 庫支援的任何摘要演算法都可供 JavaScript 程式碼使用。

``crypto.createHash(`*algorithm*`)``

` `crypto.createHash(` *演算法* `)` `

Creates and returns a [Hash](#crypto_hash) object that can be used to generate hash digests using the given `*algorithm*`. The algorithm can be `md5`, `sha1`, `sha256`, `sha384`, `sha512`, and any other digest supported by the linked OpenSSL library (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)). Before version [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>), only `md5`, `sha1`, and `sha256` were supported.

建立並傳回一個 [Hash](#crypto_hash)對象，該物件可用於使用給定的`*algorithm*`產生哈希摘要。演算法可以是`md5`, `sha1`, `sha256`, `sha384`, `sha512` ，以及連結的 OpenSSL 函式庫支援的任何其他摘要演算法（自 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)起）。在版本 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)之前，僅支援`md5`, `sha1`和`sha256` 。

``crypto.createHmac(`*algorithm*`, `*secret key*`)``

` `crypto.createHmac(` *演算法* `, ` *金鑰* `)` `

Creates and returns an [HMAC](#crypto_hmac) object that uses the given `*algorithm*` and `*secret key*`. The algorithm can be `md5`, `sha1`, `sha256`, `sha384`, `sha512`, and any other digest supported by the linked OpenSSL library (since [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)). Before version [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>), only `md5`, `sha1`, and `sha256` were supported.

建立並傳回一個使用給定的`*algorithm*`和`*secret key*`的 [HMAC](#crypto_hmac)物件。演算法可以是`md5`, `sha1`, `sha256`, `sha384`, `sha512`以及連結的 OpenSSL 函式庫支援的任何其他摘要演算法（自 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)起）。在 [0.9.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.9.7>)版本之前，僅支援`md5`, `sha1`和`sha256` 。

#### Hash｜哈希

|   |
| --- |
| [`hash.update()`](#crypto_hash_update) |
| [`hash.digest()`](#crypto_hash_digest) |

``hash.update(`*data*`)``

` `hash.update(` *數據* `)` `

Updates the hash content with the given `*data*`.

使用給定的`*data*`更新哈希內容。

``hash.digest([`*encoding*`])``

` `hash.digest([` *編碼* `])` `

Calculates the digest of all of the data passed using `hash.update()`. The encoding can be `hex`, `base64`, and `base64url`. If encoding is not provided, a Buffer object ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)) is returned.

使用`hash.update()`計算所有傳遞資料的摘要。編碼可以是`hex`, `base64`和`base64url` 。如果未提供編碼，則傳回一個 Buffer 物件 ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) )。

> Before version ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)), a byte string was returned instead of a Buffer object.
>
> 在版本 ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) ) 之前，傳回的是位元組字串而不是 Buffer 物件。

`hash.copy()`

Makes a copy of the current state of the hash (since [0.7.12](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.12>)).

複製哈希的當前狀態（因為 [0.7.12](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.12>) ）。

> ```js
> import crypto from 'crypto';
> 
> crypto.createHash('sha1').update('A').update('B').digest('base64url');
> /* BtlFlCqiamG-GMPiK_GbvKjdK10 */
> ```

#### HMAC

|   |
| --- |
| [`hmac.update()`](#crypto_hmac_update) |
| [`hmac.digest()`](#crypto_hmac_digest) |

``hmac.update(`*data*`)``

` `hmac.update(` *數據* `)` `

Updates the HMAC content with the given `*data*`.

使用給定的`*data*`更新HMAC的內容。

``hmac.digest([`*encoding*`])``

` `hmac.digest([` *編碼* `])` `

Calculates the HMAC digest of all of the data passed using `hmac.update()`. The encoding can be `hex`, `base64`, and `base64url`. If encoding is not provided, a Buffer object ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)) is returned.

使用`hmac.update()`方式HMAC計算所有傳遞資料的摘要。編碼方式可以是`hex`, `base64`和`base64url` 。如果未提供編碼方式，則傳回一個 Buffer 物件 ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) )。

> Before version [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>), a byte string was returned instead of a Buffer object.
>
> 在版本 [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)之前，傳回的是位元組字串而非 Buffer 物件。

> ```js
> import crypto from 'crypto';
> 
> crypto.createHmac('sha1', 'secret.key').update('AB').digest('base64url');
>
> crypto.createHmac('sha1', 'secret.key').update(' AB ').digest('base64url');
> /* Oglm93xn23_MkiaEq_e9u8zk374 */
> ```

| --- |
| [`fs.accessSync()`](#fs_accesssync) |

| [`fs.closeSync()`](#fs_closesync) |
| [`fs.existsSync()`](#fs_existssync) |

| [`fs.readFileSync()`](#fs_readfilesync) |
| [`fs.readlinkSync()`](#fs_readlinksync) |
| [`fs.readSync()`](#fs_readsync) |
| [`fs.realpathSync()`](#fs_realpathsync) |
| [`fs.renameSync()`](#fs_renamesync) |
| [`fs.rmdirSync()`](#fs_rmdirsync) |
| [`fs.statSync()`](#fs_statsync) |
| [`fs.symlinkSync()`](#fs_symlinksync) |
| [`fs.unlinkSync()`](#fs_unlinksync) |

| [`fs.writeSync()`](#fs_writesync_str) |

|   |
| --- |
| [`fs.Dirent`](#fs_dirent) |
| [`fs.FileHandle`](#fs_filehandle) |
| [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) |
| [`File Access Constants`](#access_const) |
| [`File System Flags`](#njs_api_fs_flags) |

The File System module provides operations with files.

文件系統模組提供檔案操作功能。

The module object is imported using `import fs from 'fs'`. Since [0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>), promissified versions of file system methods are available through `fs.promises` object after importing with `import fs from 'fs'`:

模組物件使用`import fs from 'fs'`導入。自 [0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>)起，檔案系統方法的 Promissified 版本可透過`fs.promises`物件透過`import fs from 'fs'`匯入後取得：

> ```js
> import fs from 'fs';
>
> 從 'fs' 導入 fs；
> 
> fs.promises.readFile("/file/path").then((data) => {
>     /* <file data> */
> });
> ```

``accessSync(`*path*`[, `*mode*`])``

` `accessSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *模式* `])` `

Synchronously tests permissions for a file or directory specified in the `path` ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>)). If the check fails, an error will be returned, otherwise, the method will return undefined.

同步測試`path` ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>) ) 中指定的檔案或目錄的權限。如果檢查失敗，則傳回錯誤；否則，該方法將傳回 undefined。

`mode`

an optional integer that specifies the accessibility checks to be performed, by default is [`fs.constants.F_OK`](#access_const)

一個可選的整數，用於指定要執行的輔助功能檢查，預設值為 [`fs.constants.F_OK`](#access_const)

> ```
> try {
>
> 嘗試 {
>     fs.accessSync('/file/path', fs.constants.R_OK | fs.constants.W_OK);
>     console.log('has access');
>
>     console.log('已取得存取權限');
> } catch (e) {
>     console.log('no access');)
>
>     console.log('無存取權限');)
> }
> ```

``appendFileSync(`*filename*`, `*data*`[, `*options*`])``

` `appendFileSync(` *檔案名稱* `, ` *資料* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously appends specified `data` to a file with provided `filename`. The `data` is expected to be a string or a Buffer object ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)). If the file does not exist, it will be created. The `options` parameter is expected to be an object with the following keys:

將指定的 `data` 同步追加到具有提供的 `filename` 的檔案中。 `data` 應為字串或 Buffer 物件 ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>))。如果該文件不存在，則會建立該文件。 `options`參數預計是具有以下鍵的物件：

`mode`

mode option, by default is `0o666`

模式選項，預設值為`0o666`

`flag`

file system [flag](#njs_api_fs_flags), by default is `a`

檔案系統 [標誌](#njs_api_fs_flags) ，預設值為`a`

``closeSync(`*fd*`)``

` `closeSync(` *fd* `)` `

Closes the `fd` file descriptor represented by an integer used by the method. Returns `undefined`.

關閉由該方法使用的整數表示的`fd`檔案描述符。返回`undefined` 。

``existsSync(`*path*`)``

` `existsSync(` *路徑* `)` `

Boolean value, returns `true` if the specified `*path*` exists. ([0.8.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.2>))

布林值，如果指定的`*path*`存在，則傳回`true` 。 （[0.8.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.2>) ）

``fstatSync(`*fd*`)``

` `fstatSync(` *fd* `)` `

Retrieves the [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) object for the file descriptor ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)). The `fd` parameter is an integer representing the file descriptor used by the method.

檢索文件描述子 ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)) 的 [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) 物件。 `fd`參數是一個整數，表示該方法使用的檔案描述子。

``lstatSync(`*path*`[, `*options*`])``

` `lstatSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously retrieves the [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) object for the symbolic link referred to by `path` ([0.7.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.1>)). The `options` parameter is expected to be an object with the following keys:

同步檢索 `path` ([0.7.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.1>)) 所引用的符號連結的 [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) 物件。 `options`參數預計是具有以下鍵的物件：

`throwIfNoEntry`

a boolean value which indicates whether an exception is thrown if no file system entry exists, rather than returning `undefined`, by default is `false`.

一個布林值，指示如果不存在檔案系統條目，是否拋出異常，而不是返回`undefined` ，預設值為`false` 。

``mkdirSync(`*path*`[, `*options*`])``

` `mkdirSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously creates a directory at the specified `path` ([0.4.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.2>)). The `options` parameter is expected to be an `integer` that specifies the [mode](#fs_mkdirsync_mode), or an object with the following keys:

在指定的`path`（[0.4.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.2>)）同步建立目錄。 `options`參數應為指定[模式](#fs_mkdirsync_mode)的`integer`，或具有以下鍵的物件：

`mode`

mode option, by default is `0o777`.

模式選項，預設值為`0o777` 。

``openSync(`*path*`[, `*flags*`[, `*mode*`]])``

` `openSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *標誌* `[, ` *模式* `]])` `

Returns an integer representing the file descriptor for the opened file `path` ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)).

傳回一個整數，表示已開啟檔案的檔案描述子`path` ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) )。

`flags`

file system [flag](#njs_api_fs_flags), by default is `r`

檔案系統 [標誌](#njs_api_fs_flags) ，預設值為`r`

`mode`

mode option, by default is `0o666`

模式選項，預設值為`0o666`

``promises.open(`*path*`[, `*flags*`[, `*mode*`]])``

` `promises.open(` *路徑* `[, ` *標誌* `[, ` *模式* `]])` `

Returns a [`FileHandle`](#fs_filehandle) object representing the opened file `path` ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)).

回傳一個 [`FileHandle`](#fs_filehandle)對象，表示開啟的檔案`path` ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) )。

`flags`

file system [flag](#njs_api_fs_flags), by default is `r`

檔案系統 [標誌](#njs_api_fs_flags) ，預設值為`r`

`mode`

mode option, by default is `0o666`

模式選項，預設值為`0o666`

``readdirSync(`*path*`[, `*options*`])``

` `readdirSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously reads the contents of a directory at the specified `path` ([0.4.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.2>)). The `options` parameter is expected to be a string that specifies [encoding](#fs_readdirsync_encoding) or an object with the following keys:

同步讀取指定`path`（[0.4.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.2>)）處的目錄內容。 `options` 參數應為指定 [encoding](#fs_readdirsync_encoding) 的字串或具有以下鍵的物件：

`encoding`

encoding, by default is `utf8`. The encoding can be `utf8` and `buffer` ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)).

預設編碼為`utf8` 。編碼可以是`utf8`和`buffer` （[0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) ）。

`withFileTypes`

if set to `true`, the files array will contain [`fs.Dirent`](#fs_dirent) objects, by default is `false`.

如果設定為`true` ，則檔案陣列將包含 [`fs.Dirent`](#fs_dirent)對象，預設值為`false` 。

``readFileSync(`*filename*`[, `*options*`])``

` `readFileSync(` *檔案名稱* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously returns the contents of the file with provided `filename`. The `options` parameter holds `string` that specifies encoding. If an encoding is specified, a string is returned, otherwise, a Buffer object ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)) is returned.

同步傳回指定參數`filename`下的檔案內容。參數`options`包含指定編碼的`string` 。如果指定了編碼，則傳回字串；否則，傳回 Buffer 物件 ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) )。

> Before version [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>), a [byte string](#string_tobytes) was returned if encoding was not specified.
>
> 在版本 [0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)之前，如果未指定編碼，則傳回 [位元組字串](#string_tobytes) 。

Otherwise, `options` is expected to be an object with the following keys:

否則， `options`應為具有以下鍵的物件：

`encoding`

encoding, by default is not specified. The encoding can be `utf8`, `hex` ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)), `base64` ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)), `base64url` ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)).

編碼，預設不指定。編碼可以是 `utf8`, `hex` ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)), `base64` ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)), `base64url` （[0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)）。

`flag`

file system [flag](#njs_api_fs_flags), by default is `r`

檔案系統 [標誌](#njs_api_fs_flags) ，預設值為`r`

> ```js
> import fs from 'fs';
>
> 從 'fs' 導入 fs；
> 
> var file = fs.readFileSync('/file/path.tar.gz');
> console.log(file.slice(0,2).toString('hex')) /* '1f8b' */
> ```

``readlinkSync(`*path*`[, `*options*`])``

` `readlinkSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously gets the contents of the symbolic link `path` using [readlink(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/readlink.2.html) ([0.8.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.7>)). The `options` argument can be a string specifying an encoding, or an object with `encoding` property specifying the character encoding to use. If the `encoding` is `*buffer*`, the result is returned as a `Buffer` object, otherwise as a string.

使用 [readlink(2)] 同步取得符號連結`path`的內容 ([(https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/readlink.2.html)](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.8.7>) )。參數`options`可以是指定編碼的字串，也可以是具有指定要使用的字元編碼的屬性`encoding` 0.8.7物件。如果`encoding`為`*buffer*` ，則結果以`Buffer`物件的形式傳回，否則以字串的形式傳回。

``readSync(`*fd*`, `*buffer*`, `*offset*`[, `*length*`[, `*position*`]])``

` `readSync(` *檔案描述子* `, ` *緩衝區* `, ` *偏移量* `[, ` *長度* `[, ` *位置* `]])`

Reads the content of a file path using file descriptor `fd`, returns the number of bytes read ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)).

使用檔案描述子`fd`讀取檔案路徑的內容，傳回讀取的位元組數（[0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) ）。

`buffer`

the `buffer` value can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

`buffer`值可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`offset`

is an `integer` representing the position in buffer to write the data to

`integer`表示緩衝區中要寫入資料的位置

`length`

is an `integer` representing the number of bytes to read

`integer`表示要讀取的位元組數

`position`

specifies where to begin reading from in the file, the value can be `integer` or `null`, by default is `null`. If `position` is `null`, data will be read from the current file position, and the file position will be updated. If position is an `integer`, the file position will be unchanged

指定從檔案讀取資料的起始位置，該值可以是`integer`或`null` ，預設值為`null` 。如果`position`為`null` ，則從目前檔案位置讀取數據，並且檔案位置將會更新。如果位置為`integer` ，則檔案位置將保持不變。

``realpathSync(`*path*`[, `*options*`])``

` `realpathSync(` *路徑* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously computes the canonical pathname by resolving `.`, `..` and symbolic links using [realpath(3)](http://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man3/realpath.3.html). The `options` argument can be a string specifying an encoding, or an object with an encoding property specifying the character encoding to use for the path passed to the callback ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>)).

透過解析 `.`, `..` 和使用 [realpath(3)](http://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man3/realpath.3.html) 的符號連結同步計算規範路徑名。 `options`參數可以是指定編碼的字串，也可以是具有編碼屬性的對象，該編碼屬性指定用於傳遞給回呼的路徑的字元編碼（[0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>)）。

``renameSync(`*oldPath*`, `*newPath*`)``

` `renameSync(` *舊路徑* `, ` *新路徑* `)` `

Synchronously changes the name or location of a file from `oldPath` to `newPath` ([0.3.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.4>)).

同步地將檔案名稱或位置從`oldPath`改為`newPath` ([0.3.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.4>) )。

> ```js
> import fs from 'fs';
>
> 從 'fs' 導入 fs；
> 
> fs.renameSync('hello.txt', 'HelloWorld.txt');
> ```

``rmdirSync(`*path*`)``

` `rmdirSync(` *路徑* `)` `

Synchronously removes a directory at the specified `path` ([0.4.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.2>)).

同步刪除指定位置的目錄`path` ([0.4.2](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.2>) )。

``statSync(`*path*`,[ `*options*`])``

` `statSync(` *路徑* `,[ ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously retrieves the [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) object for the specified `path` ([0.7.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.1>)). The `path` can be a `string` or `buffer`. The `options` parameter is expected to be an object with the following keys:

同步檢索指定 `path` ([0.7.1](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.1>)) 的 [`fs.Stats`](#fs_stats) 物件。 `path` 可以是`string` 或`buffer`。 `options`參數預計是具有以下鍵的物件：

`throwIfNoEntry`

a boolean value which indicates whether an exception is thrown if no file system entry exists rather than returning `undefined`, by default is `true`.

一個布林值，指示如果不存在檔案系統條目，是否拋出異常而不是返回`undefined` ，預設值為`true` 。

``symlinkSync(`*target*`, `*path*`)``

` `symlinkSync(` *目標* `, ` *路徑* `)` `

Synchronously creates the link called `path` pointing to `target` using [symlink(2)](http://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/symlink.2.html) ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>)). Relative targets are relative to the link’s parent directory.

使用 [symlink(2)](http://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/symlink.2.html) ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>) ) 同步建立指向`path`的名為`target`的連結。相對目標是相對於連結的父目錄而言的。

``unlinkSync(`*path*`)``

` `unlinkSync(` *路徑* `)` `

Synchronously unlinks a file by `path` ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>)).

透過`path` ([0.3.9](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.3.9>) ) 同步取消連結檔。

``writeFileSync(`*filename*`, `*data*`[, `*options*`])``

` `writeFileSync(` *檔案名稱* `, ` *資料* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Synchronously writes `data` to a file with provided `filename`. The `data` is expected to be a string or a Buffer object ([0.4.4](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>)). If the file does not exist, it will be created, if the file exists, it will be replaced. The `options` parameter is expected to be an object with the following keys:

將`data`同步寫入指定檔案`filename`應為字串或 Buffer 物件（[`data`](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.4>) ）。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件；如果文件已存在，則會取代該文件`options`參數應為包含以下鍵0.4.4物件：

`mode`

mode option, by default is `0o666`

模式選項，預設值為`0o666`

`flag`

file system [flag](#njs_api_fs_flags), by default is `w`

檔案系統 [標誌](#njs_api_fs_flags) ，預設值為`w`

> ```js
> import fs from 'fs';
>
> 從 'fs' 導入 fs；
> 
> fs.writeFileSync('hello.txt', 'Hello world');
> ```

``writeSync(`*fd*`, `*buffer*`, `*offset*`[, `*length*`[, `*position*`]])``

` `writeSync(` *檔案描述子* `, ` *緩衝區* `, ` *偏移量* `[, ` *長度* `[, ` *位置* `]])`

Writes a buffer to a file using file descriptor, returns the `number` of bytes written ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)).

使用文件描述子將緩衝區寫入文件，傳回寫入的位元組數`number` ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) )。

`fd`

an `integer` representing the file descriptor

`integer`表示檔案描述符

`buffer`

the `buffer` value can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

`buffer`值可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`offset`

is an `integer` that determines the part of the buffer to be written, by default `0`

`integer`用於確定要寫入的緩衝區部分，預設值為`0`

`length`

is an `integer` specifying the number of bytes to write, by default is an offset of [Buffer.byteLength](#buffer_bytelength)

`integer`指定要寫入的位元組數，預設值為 [Buffer.byteLength 的偏移量](#buffer_bytelength)

`position`

refers to the offset from the beginning of the file where this data should be written, can be an `integer` or `null`, by default is `null`. See also [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html).

指的是從檔案開頭到寫入此資料的偏移量，可以是`integer`或`null` ，預設值為`null` 。另請參閱 [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html) 。

``writeSync(`*fd*`, `*string*`[, `*position*`[, `*encoding*`]])``

` `writeSync(` *fd* `, ` *string* `[, ` *position* `[, ` *encoding* `]])` `

Writes a `string` to a file using file descriptor `fd`, returns the `number` of bytes written ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)).

使用檔案描述子`string`向檔案寫入`fd` ，返回寫入的位元組數`number` ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>) )。

`fd`

is an `integer` representing a file descriptor

是一個`integer` ，表示檔案描述符

`position`

refers to the offset from the beginning of the file where this data should be written, can be an `integer` or `null`, by default is `null`. See also [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html)

指的是資料應寫入的檔案起始位置的偏移量，可以是`integer`或`null` ，預設值為`null` 。另請參閱 [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html)

`encoding`

is a `string`, by default is `utf8`

是`string` ，預設值為`utf8`

#### fs.Dirent

`fs.Dirent` is a representation of a directory entry — a file or a subdirectory. When [`readdirSync()`](#fs_readdirsync) is called with the [`withFileTypes`](#fs_readdirsync_withfiletypes) option, the resulting array contains `fs.Dirent` objects.

`fs.Dirent`表示目錄項目－檔案或子目錄。當使用 [`readdirSync()`](#fs_readdirsync)選項呼叫 [`withFileTypes`](#fs_readdirsync_withfiletypes)時，結果陣列包含`fs.Dirent`個物件。

-   `dirent.isBlockDevice()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a block device.  
    `dirent.isBlockDevice()` — 若`fs.Dirent`物件描述塊設備，則回傳`true` 。
-   `dirent.isCharacterDevice()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a character device.  
    `dirent.isCharacterDevice()` — 如果`fs.Dirent`物件描述字元設備，則傳回`true` 。
-   `dirent.isDirectory()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a file system directory.  
    `dirent.isDirectory()` — 如果`fs.Dirent`物件描述檔案系統目錄，則回傳`true` 。
-   `dirent.isFIFO()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a first-in-first-out (FIFO) pipe.  
    `dirent.isFIFO()` — 若`fs.Dirent`物件描述一個先進先出 ( FIFO ) 管道，則回傳`true` 。
-   `dirent.isFile()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a regular file.  
    `dirent.isFile()` — 如果`fs.Dirent`物件描述的是常規文件，則回傳`true` 。
-   `dirent.isSocket()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a socket.  
    `dirent.isSocket()` — 若`fs.Dirent`物件描述一個套接字，則回傳`true` 。
-   `dirent.isSymbolicLink()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Dirent` object describes a symbolic link.  
    `dirent.isSymbolicLink()` — 如果`fs.Dirent`物件描述符號鏈接，則返回`true` 。
-   `dirent.name` — the name of the file `fs.Dirent` object refers to.  
    `dirent.name` — `fs.Dirent`物件所引用的檔案的名稱。

#### fs.FileHandle｜fs.檔案句柄

|   |
| --- |
| [`filehandle.close()`](#filehandle_close) |
| [`filehandle.fd`](#filehandle_fd) |
| [`filehandle.read()`](#filehandle_read) |
| [`filehandle.stat()`](#filehandle_stat) |
| [``filehandle.write(`*buf*`)``](#filehandle_write_buf)<br>[` `filehandle.write(` *buf* `)` `](#filehandle_write_buf) |
| [``filehandle.write(`*str*`)``](#filehandle_write_str)<br>[` `filehandle.write(` *str* `)` `](#filehandle_write_str) |

The `FileHandle` object is an object wrapper for a numeric file descriptor ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)). Instances of the `FileHandle` object are created by the [`fs.promises.open()`](#fs_promises_open) method. If a `FileHandle` is not closed using the [`filehandle.close()`](#filehandle_close) method, it will try to automatically close the file descriptor, helping to prevent memory leaks. Please do not rely on this behavior because it can be unreliable. Instead, always explicitly close a `FileHandle`.

`FileHandle` 物件是數位檔案描述子 ([0.7.7](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.7>)) 的物件包裝器。 `FileHandle`物件的實例由[`fs.promises.open()`](#fs_promises_open)方法所建立。如果沒有使用[`filehandle.close()`](#filehandle_close)方法關閉`FileHandle`，它將嘗試自動關閉檔案描述符，有助於防止記憶體洩漏。請不要依賴此行為，因為它可能不可靠。相反，始終明確關閉 `FileHandle`。

`filehandle.close()`

Closes the file handle after waiting for any pending operation on the handle to complete. Returns a `promise`, fulfills with undefined upon success.

等待句柄上所有待處理的操作完成後，關閉檔案句柄。回傳一個`promise` ，成功時回傳 undefined。

`filehandle.fd`

The numeric file descriptor managed by the `FileHandle` object.

由`FileHandle`物件管理的數位檔案描述符。

``filehandle.read(`*buffer*`, `*offset*`[, `*length*`[, `*position*`]])``

` `filehandle.read(` *緩衝區* `, ` *偏移量* `[, ` *長度* `[, ` *位置* `]])` `

Reads data from the file and stores that in the given buffer.

從檔案中讀取資料並將其儲存在給定的緩衝區中。

`buffer`

a buffer that will be filled with the file data read, the value can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

一個用於填充讀取的檔案資料的緩衝區，其值可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`offset`

is an `integer` representing the location in the buffer at which to start filling

`integer`表示緩衝區中開始填滿的位置

`length`

is an `integer` representing the number of bytes to read

`integer`表示要讀取的位元組數

`position`

the location where to begin reading data from the file, the value can be `integer`, `null`. If `null`, data will be read from the current file position and the position will be updated. If position is an `integer`, the current file position will remain unchanged.

指定從檔案讀取資料的起始位置，其值為`integer`, `null` 。如果`null` ，則從目前檔案位置讀取資料並更新該位置。如果值為`integer` ，則目前檔案位置保持不變。

Returns a `Promise` which fulfills upon success with an object with two properties:

傳回一個 `Promise`，它在成功時滿足具有兩個屬性的物件：

`bytesRead`

is an `integer` representing the number of bytes read

`integer`表示讀取的位元組數

`buffer`

is a reference to the passed argument in buffer, can be `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

是對緩衝區中傳遞的參數的引用，可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`filehandle.stat()`

Fulfills with an [fs.Stats](#fs_stats) for the file, returns a `promise`.

使用 [fs.Stats](#fs_stats)填滿文件，傳回`promise` 。

``filehandle.write(`*buffer*`, `*offset*`[, `*length*`[, `*position*`]])``

` `filehandle.write(` *緩衝區* `, ` *偏移量* `[, ` *長度* `[, ` *位置* `]])` `

Writes a buffer to the file.

將緩衝區內容寫入檔案。

`buffer`

the `buffer` value can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

`buffer`值可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

`offset`

is an `integer` representing the start position from within buffer where the data to write begins

`integer`表示緩衝區內要寫入的資料的起始位置。

`length`

is an `integer` representing the number of bytes from buffer to write, by default is an offset of [Buffer.byteLength](#buffer_bytelength)

`integer`表示要寫入的緩衝區位元組數，預設值為 [Buffer.byteLength 的偏移量](#buffer_bytelength)

`position`

the offset from the beginning of the file where the data from buffer should be written, can be an `integer` or `null`, by default is `null`. If `position` is not a `number`, the data will be written at the current position. See the POSIX [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html) documentation for details.

緩衝區中的資料應寫入的檔案起始位置的偏移量，可以是`integer`或`null` ，預設值為`null` 。如果`position`不是`number` ，則資料將寫入目前位置。有關詳細信息，請參閱POSIX [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html)文件。

Returns a `Promise` which is resolved with an object containing two properties:

傳回一個`Promise`該物件解析後包含兩個屬性：

`bytesWritten`

is an `integer` representing the number of bytes written

是一個`integer` ，表示寫入的位元組數。

`buffer`

a reference to the buffer written, can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

已寫入緩衝區的引用，可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

> It is unsafe to use `filehandle.write()` multiple times on the same file without waiting for the promise to be resolved or rejected.
>
> 在不等待 promise 被解決或拒絕的情況下，對同一個文件多次使用`filehandle.write()`是不安全的。

``filehandle.write(`*string*`[, `*position*`[, `*encoding*`]])``

` `filehandle.write(` *字串* `[, ` *位置* `[, ` *編碼* `]])` `

Writes a `string` to the file.

向文件中寫入`string` 。

`position`

the offset from the beginning of the file where the data from buffer should be written, can be an `integer` or `null`, by default is `null`. If `position` is not a `number`, the data will be written at the current position. See the POSIX [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html) documentation for details.

緩衝區中的資料應寫入的檔案起始位置的偏移量，可以是`integer`或`null` ，預設值為`null` 。如果`position`不是`number` ，則資料將寫入目前位置。有關詳細信息，請參閱POSIX [pwrite(2)](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/write.2.html)文件。

`encoding`

the expected encoding of the string, by default `utf8`

字串的預期編碼，預設值為`utf8`

Returns a `Promise` which is resolved with an object containing two properties:

傳回一個`Promise`該物件解析為包含兩個屬性的物件：

`bytesWritten`

is an `integer` representing the number of bytes written

`integer`表示寫入的位元組數

`buffer`

a reference to the buffer written, can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`

已寫入緩衝區的引用，可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView`

> It is unsafe to use `filehandle.write()` multiple times on the same file without waiting for the promise to be resolved or rejected.
>
> 在不等待 Promise 被解決或拒絕的情況下，對同一個文件多次使用`filehandle.write()`是不安全的。

#### fs.Stats

The `fs.Stats` object provides information about a file. The object is returned from [fs.statSync()](#fs_statsync) and [fs.lstatSync()](#fs_lstatsync).

`fs.Stats`物件提供有關文件的資訊。此物件由 [fs.statSync()](#fs_statsync)和 [fs.lstatSync()](#fs_lstatsync)傳回。

-   `stats.isBlockDevice()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Stats` object describes a block device.  
    `stats.isBlockDevice()` — 若`fs.Stats`物件描述塊設備，則回傳`true` 。
-   `stats.isDirectory()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Stats` object describes a file system directory.  
    `stats.isDirectory()` — 如果`fs.Stats`物件描述檔案系統目錄，則回傳`true` 。
-   `stats.isFIFO()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Stats` object describes a first-in-first-out (FIFO) pipe.  
    `stats.isFIFO()` — 若`fs.Stats`物件描述一個先進先出 ( FIFO ) 管道，則回傳`true` 。
-   `stats.isFile()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Stats` object describes a regular file.  
    `stats.isFile()` — 如果`fs.Stats`物件描述的是常規文件，則回傳`true` 。
-   `stats.isSocket()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Stats` object describes a socket.  
    `stats.isSocket()` — 如果`fs.Stats`物件描述一個套接字，則回傳`true` 。
-   `stats.isSymbolicLink()` — returns `true` if the `fs.Stats` object describes a symbolic link.  
    `stats.isSymbolicLink()` — 如果`fs.Stats`物件描述符號鏈接，則返回`true` 。
-   `stats.dev` — the numeric identifier of the device containing the file.  
    `stats.dev` — 包含該檔案的裝置的數字識別碼。
-   `stats.ino` — the file system specific `Inode` number for the file.  
    `stats.ino` — 檔案系統特定的`Inode`檔案編號。
-   `stats.mode` — a bit-field describing the file type and mode.  
    `stats.mode` — 描述檔案類型和模式的位元域。
-   `stats.nlink` — the number of hard-links that exist for the file.  
    `stats.nlink` — 檔案存在的硬連結數量。
-   `stats.uid` — the numeric user identifier of the user that owns the file (POSIX).  
    `stats.uid` — 檔案擁有者的數位使用者識別碼 ( POSIX )。
-   `stats.gid` — the numeric group identifier of the group that owns the file (POSIX).  
    `stats.gid` — 擁有該檔案的群組的數字群組標識符 ( POSIX )。
-   `stats.rdev` — the numeric device identifier if the file represents a device.  
    `stats.rdev` — 如果檔案代表一個設備，則為該設備的數字識別碼。
-   `stats.size` — the size of the file in bytes.  
    `stats.size` — 檔案大小（以位元組為單位）。
-   `stats.blksize` — the file system block size for i/o operations.  
    `stats.blksize` — 檔案系統 I/O 操作的區塊大小。
-   `stats.blocks` — the number of blocks allocated for this file.  
    `stats.blocks` — 為此檔案所分配的區塊數。
-   `stats.atimeMs` — the timestamp indicating the last time this file was accessed expressed in milliseconds since the POSIX Epoch.  
    `stats.atimeMs` — 表示自POSIX紀元以來，最後一次存取此檔案的時間戳記（以毫秒為單位）。
-   `stats.mtimeMs` — the timestamp indicating the last time this file was modified expressed in milliseconds since the POSIX Epoch.  
    `stats.mtimeMs` — 表示自POSIX紀元以來，此文件最後修改時間的時間戳記（以毫秒為單位）。
-   `stats.ctimeMs` — the timestamp indicating the last time this file was changed expressed in milliseconds since the POSIX Epoch.  
    `stats.ctimeMs` — 表示自POSIX紀元以來，該檔案上次更改的時間戳（以毫秒為單位）。
-   `stats.birthtimeMs` — the timestamp indicating the creation time of this file expressed in milliseconds since the POSIX Epoch.  
    `stats.birthtimeMs` — 表示此檔案建立時間的時間戳，以自POSIX紀元以來的毫秒數表示。
-   `stats.atime` — the timestamp indicating the last time this file was accessed.  
    `stats.atime` — 表示上次存取此文件的時間戳記。
-   `stats.mtime` — the timestamp indicating the last time this file was modified.  
    `stats.mtime` — 表示此檔案上次修改時間的時間戳記。
-   `stats.ctime` — the timestamp indicating the last time this file was changed.  
    `stats.ctime` — 表示此檔案上次更改時間的時間戳記。
-   `stats.birthtime` — the timestamp indicating the creation time of this file.  
    `stats.birthtime` — 表示此檔案建立時間的時間戳記。

#### File Access Constants｜文件存取常量

The [`access()`](#fs_accesssync) method can accept the following flags. These flags are exported by `fs.constants`:

[`access()`](#fs_accesssync)方法可以接受以下標誌。這些標誌由`fs.constants`導出：

-   `F_OK` — indicates that the file is visible to the calling process, used by default if no mode is specified  
    `F_OK` — 表示檔案對呼叫進程可見，如果未指定模式，則預設使用此設定。
-   `R_OK` — indicates that the file can be read by the calling process  
    `R_OK` — 表示呼叫進程可以讀取該文件
-   `W_OK` — indicates that the file can be written by the calling process  
    `W_OK` — 表示呼叫進程可以寫入該文件
-   `X_OK` — indicates that the file can be executed by the calling process  
    `X_OK` — 表示該檔案可被呼叫進程執行

#### File System Flags｜檔案系統標誌

The `flag` option can accept the following values:

`flag`選項可以接受以下值：

-   `a` — open a file for appending. The file is created if it does not exist  
    `a` — 開啟一個檔案以追加寫入。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件。
-   `ax` — the same as `a` but fails if the file already exists  
    `ax` — 與`a`相同，但若檔案已存在則會失敗
-   `a+` — open a file for reading and appending. If the file does not exist, it will be created  
    `a+` — 開啟一個檔案進行讀取和追加操作。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件。
-   `ax+` — the same as `a+` but fails if the file already exists  
    `ax+` — 與`a+`相同，但若檔案已存在則失敗
-   `as` — open a file for appending in synchronous mode. If the file does not exist, it will be created  
    `as` — 以同步模式開啟檔案進行追加寫入。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件。
-   `as+` — open a file for reading and appending in synchronous mode. If the file does not exist, it will be created  
    `as+` — 以同步模式開啟檔案進行讀取與追加操作。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件。
-   `r` — open a file for reading. An exception occurs if the file does not exist  
    `r` — 開啟檔案進行讀取。如果文件不存在，則會發生異常。
-   `r+` — open a file for reading and writing. An exception occurs if the file does not exist  
    `r+` — 開啟檔案進行讀寫操作。如果文件不存在，則會發生異常。
-   `rs+` — open a file for reading and writing in synchronous mode. Instructs the operating system to bypass the local file system cache  
    `rs+` — 以同步模式開啟檔案進行讀寫操作。指示作業系統繞過本機檔案系統快取。
-   `w` — open a file for writing. If the file does not exist, it will be created. If the file exists, it will be replaced  
    `w` — 開啟一個檔案進行寫入。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件；如果文件已存在，則會取代該文件。
-   `wx` — the same as `w` but fails if the file already exists  
    `wx` — 與`w`相同，但若檔案已存在則會失敗
-   `w+` — open a file for reading and writing. If the file does not exist, it will be created. If the file exists, it will be replaced  
    `w+` — 開啟一個檔案進行讀寫操作。如果文件不存在，則會建立該文件；如果文件已存在，則會取代該文件。
-   `wx+` — the same as `w+` but fails if the file already exists  
    `wx+` — 與`w+`相同，但若檔案已存在則會失敗

#### Query String｜查詢字串

|   |
| --- |
| [`querystring.decode()`](#querystring_decode) |
| [`querystring.encode()`](#querystring_encode) |
| [`querystring.escape()`](#querystring_escape) |
| [`querystring.parse()`](#querystring_parse) |
| [`querystring.stringify()`](#querystring_stringify) |
| [`querystring.unescape()`](#querystring_unescape) |

The Query String module provides support for parsing and formatting URL query strings ([0.4.3](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.4.3>)). The Query String module object is imported using `import qs from 'querystring'`.

查詢字串模組提供對解析和格式化查詢URL （ 0.4.3 (https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/changes.html#njs0.4.3) ）的支援。 `import qs from 'querystring'`字串模組物件使用 。

`querystring.decode()`

is an alias for [`querystring.parse()`](#querystring_parse).

是 [`querystring.parse()`](#querystring_parse)的別名。

`querystring.encode()`

is an alias for [`querystring.stringify()`](#querystring_stringify).

是 [`querystring.stringify()`](#querystring_stringify)的別名。

``querystring.escape(`*string*`)``

` `querystring.escape(` *字串* `)` `

Performs URL encoding of the given `string`, returns an escaped query string. The method is used by [`querystring.stringify()`](#querystring_stringify) and should not be used directly.

對給定的URL執行`string`編碼，傳回轉義後的查詢字串。此方法由 [`querystring.stringify()`](#querystring_stringify)使用，不應直接使用。

``querystring.parse(`*string*`[, `*separator*`[, `*equal*`[, `*options*`]]])``

` `querystring.parse(` *字串* `[, ` *分隔符號* `[, ` *相等* `[, ` *選項* `]]])` `

Parses the query string URL and returns an object.

解析查詢字串URL並傳回一個物件。

The `separator` parameter is a substring for delimiting key and value pairs in the query string, by default is “`&`”.

`separator`參數是用來分隔查詢字串中的鍵值對的子字串，預設值為「 `&` 」。

The `equal` parameter is a substring for delimiting keys and values in the query string, by default is “`=`”.

`equal`參數是用來分隔查詢字串中的鍵和值的子字串，預設值為「 `=` 」。

The `options` parameter is expected to be an object with the following keys:

`options`參數應為具有以下鍵的物件：

`decodeURIComponent` `*function*`

Function used to decode percent-encoded characters in the query string, by default is [`querystring.unescape()`](#querystring_unescape)

用於解碼查詢字串中百分比編碼字元的函數，預設值為 [`querystring.unescape()`](#querystring_unescape)

`maxKeys` `*number*`

the maximum number of keys to parse, by default is `1000`. The `0` value removes limitations for counting keys.

預設情況下，要解析`0`最大鍵數為`1000`值取消了鍵數的限制。

By default, percent-encoded characters within the query string are assumed to use the UTF-8 encoding, invalid UTF-8 sequences will be replaced with the `U+FFFD` replacement character.

預設情況下，查詢字串中的百分比編碼字元假定使用UTF -8 編碼，無效的UTF -8 序列將被替換為`U+FFFD`替換字元。

For example, for the following query string

例如，對於以下查詢字串

> ```
> 'foo=bar&abc=xyz&abc=123'
> ```

the output will be:

輸出結果為：

> ```
> {
>   foo: 'bar',
>
>   foo:'bar'，
>   abc: ['xyz', '123']
> }
> ```

``querystring.stringify(`*object*`[, `*separator*`[, `*equal*`[, `*options*`]]])``

` `querystring.stringify(` *物件* `[, ` *分隔符號* `[, ` *相等* `[, ` *選項* `]]])` `

Serializes an object and returns a URL query string.

序列化物件並傳回URL查詢字串。

The `separator` parameter is a substring for delimiting key and value pairs in the query string, by default is “`&`”.

`separator`參數是用來分隔查詢字串中的鍵和值對的子字串，預設值為「 `&` 」。

The `equal` parameter is a substring for delimiting keys and values in the query string, by default is “`=`”.

`equal`參數是用來分隔查詢字串中的鍵和值的子字串，預設值為「 `=` 」。

The `options` parameter is expected to be an object with the following keys:

`options`參數應為具有以下鍵的物件：

`encodeURIComponent` `*function*`

The function to use when converting URL-unsafe characters to percent-encoding in the query string, by default is [`querystring.escape()`](#querystring_escape).

將查詢字串中的URL不安全字元轉換為百分比編碼時要使用的函數，預設為 [`querystring.escape()`](#querystring_escape) 。

By default, characters that require percent-encoding within the query string are encoded as UTF-8. If other encoding is required, then `encodeURIComponent` option should be specified.

預設情況下，查詢字串中需要百分比編碼的字元編碼為UTF -8。如果需要其他編碼，則應指定`encodeURIComponent`選項。

For example, for the following command

例如，對於以下命令

> ```
> querystring.stringify({ foo: 'bar', baz: ['qux', 'quux'], 123: '' });
> ```

the query string will be:

查詢字串將為：

> ```
> 'foo=bar&baz=qux&baz=quux&123='
>
> 'foo=bar&base=base&base=base&123='
> ```

``querystring.unescape(`*string*`)``

` `querystring.unescape(` *字串* `)` `

Performs decoding of URL percent-encoded characters of the `string`, returns an unescaped query string. The method is used by [`querystring.parse()`](#querystring_parse) and should not be used directly.

對`string`中URL的百分比編碼字元進行解碼，傳回未轉義的查詢字串。此方法由 [`querystring.parse()`](#querystring_parse)使用，不應直接使用。

#### XML

|   |
| --- |
| [`xml.parse()`](#xml_parse) |
| [`xml.c14n()`](#xml_c14n) |
| [`xml.exclusiveC14n()`](#xml_exclusivec14n) |
| [`xml.serialize()`](#xml_serialize) |
| [`xml.serializeToString()`](#xml_serialize_tostring) |
| [`XMLDoc`](#xml_doc) |
| [`XMLNode`](#xml_node) |
| [`XMLAttr`](#xml_xmlattr) |

The XML module allows working with XML documents (since [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>)). The XML module object is imported using `import xml from 'xml'`.

XML 模組允許使用 XML 文件（自 [0.7.10](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.10>) 起）。 XML 模組物件是使用 `import xml from 'xml'` 導入的。

Example:

例子：

> ```js
> import xml from 'xml';
> 
> let data = `<note><to b="bar" a= "foo" >Tove</to><from>Jani</from></note>`;
>
> let data = `<note><to b="bar" a= "foo" >Tove</to><from>Jani</from></note>` ;
> let doc = xml.parse(data);
> 
> console.log(doc.note.to.$text) /* 'Tove' */
> console.log(doc.note.to.$attr$b) /* 'bar' */
> console.log(doc.note.$tags[1].$text) /* 'Jani' */
> 
> let dec = new TextDecoder();
> let c14n = dec.decode(xml.exclusiveC14n(doc.note));
> console.log(c14n) /* '<note><to a="foo" b="bar">Tove</to><from>Jani</from></note>' */
>
> console.log(c14n) /* ' <note><to a="foo" b="bar"> Tove </to><from> Jani </from></note> ' */
> 
> c14n = dec.decode(xml.exclusiveC14n(doc.note.to));
> console.log(c14n) /* '<to a="foo" b="bar">Tove</to>' */
>
> console.log(c14n) /* ' <to a="foo" b="bar"> Tove </to> ' */
> 
> c14n = dec.decode(xml.exclusiveC14n(doc.note, doc.note.to /* excluding 'to' */));
>
> c14n = dec.decode(xml.exclusiveC14n(doc.note, doc.note.to /* 排除 'to' */));
> console.log(c14n) /* '<note><from>Jani</from></note>' */
>
> console.log(c14n) /* ' <note><from> Jani </from></note> ' */
> ```

``parse(`*string*` | `*Buffer*`)``

` `parse(` *字串* ` | ` *緩衝區* `)` `

Parses a string or Buffer for an XML document, returns an [`XMLDoc`](#xml_doc) wrapper object representing the parsed XML document.

解析字串或XML中的文檔，傳回表示(https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/reference.html#xml_doc) `XMLDoc` XML的包裝物件。

``c14n(`*root_node*`[, `*excluding_node*`])``

` `c14n(` *根節點* `[, ` *排除節點* `])` `

Canonicalizes `root_node` and its children according to [Canonical XML Version 1.1](https://www.w3.org/TR/xml-c14n). The `root_node` can be [`XMLNode`](#xml_node) or [`XMLDoc`](#xml_doc) wrapper object around XML structure. Returns Buffer object that contains canonicalized output.

根據 [Canonical XML Version 1.1](https://www.w3.org/TR/xml-c14n) 規範化 `root_node` 及其子項。 `root_node` 可以是 [`XMLNode`](#xml_node) 或 [`XMLDoc`](#xml_doc) XML 結構的包裝物件。傳回包含規範化輸出的 Buffer 物件。

`excluding_node`

allows omitting from the output a part of the document

允許從輸出中省略文件的一部分。

``exclusiveC14n(`*root_node*`[, `*excluding_node*`[, `*withComments*` [,`*prefix_list*`]]])``

` `exclusiveC14n(` *根節點* `[, ` *排除節點* `[, ` *附註解* ` [,` *前綴列表* `]]])` `

Canonicalizes `root_node` and its children according to [Exclusive XML Canonicalization Version 1.0](https://www.w3.org/TR/xml-exc-c14n/).

根據 [獨家XML規範化版本1.0](https://www.w3.org/TR/xml-exc-c14n/)將`root_node`及其子項規範化。

`root_node`

is [`XMLNode`](#xml_node) or [`XMLDoc`](#xml_doc) wrapper object around XML structure

[`XMLNode`](#xml_node)或 [`XMLDoc`](#xml_doc)是圍繞XML結構的包裝對象

`excluding_node`

allows omitting from the output a part of the document corresponding to the node and its children

允許從輸出中省略文件中對應於節點及其子節點的部分。

`withComments`

a boolean value, `false` by default. If `true`, canonicalization corresponds to [Exclusive XML Canonicalization Version 1.0](http://www.w3.org/2001/10/xml-exc-c14n#WithComments). Returns Buffer object that contains canonicalized output.

布林值，預設值為`false` 。若為`true` ，則規範化對應於[獨佔XML規範化版本1.0](http://www.w3.org/2001/10/xml-exc-c14n#WithComments) 。傳回包含規範化輸出的 Buffer 物件。

`prefix_list`

an optional string with a space separated namespace prefixes for namespaces that should also be included into the output

一個可選字串，其中包含以空格分隔的命名空間前綴，用於指定應包含在輸出中的命名空間。

`serialize()`

The same as [`xml.c14n()`](#xml_c14n) (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)).

與 [`xml.c14n()`](#xml_c14n)相同（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）。

`serializeToString()`

The same as [`xml.c14n()`](#xml_c14n) except it returns the result as a `string` (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)).

與 [`xml.c14n()`](#xml_c14n)相同，只是回傳結果為`string` （因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）。

`XMLDoc`

An XMLDoc wrapper object around XML structure, the root node of the document.

圍繞XML結構（文檔的根節點）的 XMLDoc 包裝物件。

`doc.$root`

the document's root by its name or undefined

文件的根目錄，依名稱尋找或未定義

`` doc.`*abc*` ``

` ` doc.` *abc* ` ` `

the first root tag named `*abc*` as [`XMLNode`](#xml_node) wrapper object

第一個根標籤名為`*abc*`作為 [`XMLNode`](#xml_node)包裝對象

`XMLNode`

An XMLNode wrapper object around XML tag node.

圍繞XML標籤節點的 XMLNode 包裝物件。

`` node.`*abc*` ``

` ` node.` *abc* ` ` `

the same as [`` node.$tag$`*abc*` ``](#node_tag)

與 [` ` node.$tag$` *abc* ` ` `](#node_tag)相同

`` node.$attr$`*abc*` ``

` ` node.$attr$` *abc* ` ` `

the node's attribute value of `*abc*`, writable since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)

節點的屬性值為`*abc*` ，自 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)起可寫

`` node.$attr$`*abc*` ``\=`*xyz*`

` ` node.$attr$` *abc* ` ``\=` *xyz*`

the same as [``node.setAttribute('`*abc*`', `*xyz*`)``](#node_setattribute) (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>))

與 [` `node.setAttribute('` *abc* `', ` *xyz* `)` `](#node_setattribute)相同（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）

`node.$attrs`

an [`XMLAttr`](#xml_xmlattr) wrapper object for all attributes of the node

節點所有屬性的 [`XMLAttr`](#xml_xmlattr)包裝對象

`node.$name`

the name of the node

節點的名稱

`node.$ns`

the namespace of the node

節點的命名空間

`node.$parent`

the parent node of the current node

當前節點的父節點

`` node.$tag$`*abc*` ``

` ` node.$tag$` *abc* ` ` `

the first child tag of the node named `*abc*`, writable since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)

名為`*abc*`節點的第一個子標籤，自 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)起可寫

`node.$tags`

an array of all children tags

包含所有子標籤的數組

`node.$tags = [node1, node2, ...]`

the same as [`node.removeChildren`()](#node_removechildren); [``node.addChild(`*node1*`)``](#node_addchild); [``node.addChild(`*node2*`)``](#node_addchild) (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)).

0.7.11 [`node.removeChildren` ()](#node_removechildren)相同；[` `node.addChild(` *node1* `)``](#node_addchild); [``node.addChild(` *node2* `)` `](#node_addchild) (https://nginx.org/en/docs/njs/changes.html#njs0.7.11) ）。

`` node.$tags$`*abc*` ``

` ` node.$tags$` *abc* ` ` `

all children tags named `*abc*` of the node, writable since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)

節點的所有名為`*abc*`的子標籤，自 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)起可寫

`node.$text`

the content of the node, writable since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)

節點的內容，自 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)起可寫

`node.$text = 'abc'`

the same as [`node.setText('abc')`](#node_settext) (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>))

與 [`node.setText('abc')`](#node_settext)相同（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）

``node.addChild(`*nd*`)``

` `node.addChild(` *nd* `)` `

adds XMLNode as a child to node (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)). `nd` is recursively copied before adding to the node

將 XMLNode 新增為節點的子節點（自 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) 起）。 `nd` 在加入到節點之前遞歸複製

`node.removeAllAttributes()`

removes all attributes of the node (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>))

移除節點的所有屬性（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）

``node.removeAttribute(`*attr_name*`)``

` `node.removeAttribute(` *attr_name* `)` `

removes the attribute named `attr_name` (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>))

移除名為`attr_name`的屬性（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）

``node.removeChildren(`*tag_name*`)``

` `node.removeChildren(` *標籤名稱* `)` `

removes all the children tags named `tag_name` (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)). If `tag_name` is absent, all children tags are removed

移除所有名為`tag_name`的子標籤（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)存在）。如果`tag_name`不存在，則移除所有子標籤。

`node.removeText()`

removes the node's text value ([0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>))

移除節點的文字值（[0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）

``node.setAttribute(`*attr_name*`, `*value*`)``

` `node.setAttribute(` *attr_name* `, ` *value* `)` `

sets a value for an `attr_name` (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)). When the value is `null`, the attribute named `attr_name` is deleted

設定`attr_name`的值（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）。當值為`null`時，名為`attr_name`的屬性將會被刪除。

``node.setText(`*value*`)``

` `node.setText(` *值* `)` `

sets a text value for the node (since [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>)). When the value is `null`, the text of the node is deleted.

為節點設定文字值（因為 [0.7.11](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.11>) ）。當值為`null`時，刪除節點的文字。

`XMLAttr`

An XMLAttrs wrapper object around XML node attributes.

圍繞XML節點屬性的XMLAttrs包裝物件。

`` attr.`*abc*` ``

` ` attr.` *abc* ` ` `

the attribute value of `*abc*`

`*abc*`的屬性值

#### zlib

|   |
| --- |
| [`zlib.deflateRawSync()`](#zlib_deflaterawsync) |
| [`zlib.deflateSync()`](#zlib_deflatesync) |
| [`zlib.inflateRawSync()`](#zlib_inflaterawsync) |
| [`zlib.inflateSync()`](#zlib_inflatesync) |

The zlib module provides compression functionality using the “deflate” and “inflate” algorithms (since [0.7.12](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.12>)). The zlib module object is imported using `import zlib from 'zlib'`.

zlib 模組使用「deflate」和「inflate」演算法提供壓縮功能（自 [0.7.12](<129 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] 變化.md#njs0.7.12>)起）。 zlib 模組物件使用`import zlib from 'zlib'`導入。

``deflateRawSync(`*string*` | `*Buffer*`[, `*options*`])``

` `deflateRawSync(` *字串* ` | ` *緩衝區* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Compresses data using the “deflate” algorithm provided as a string or Buffer and does not append a zlib header. The buffer value can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`. `Options` is an optional object that contains [zlib\_options](#zlib_options). Returns Buffer instance that contains the compressed data.

使用以字串或緩衝區形式提供的“deflate”演算法壓縮數據，並且不附加 zlib 標頭。緩衝區值可以是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`，或是`DataView`. `Options`是包含[zlib\_options](#zlib_options)的選用物件。傳回包含壓縮資料的 Buffer 實例。

``deflateSync(`*string*` | `*Buffer*`[, `*options*`])``

` `deflateSync(` *字串* ` | ` *緩衝區* `[, ` *選項* `])` `

Compresses data using the “deflate” algorithm provided as a string or Buffer. The Buffer value can be a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`. `Options` is an optional object that contains [zlib\_options](#zlib_options). Returns Buffer instance that contains the compressed data.

使用以字串或緩衝區形式提供的“deflate”演算法壓縮資料。 Buffer 值可以是 `Buffer`, `TypedArray`，或 `DataView`. `Options` 是包含 [zlib\_options](#zlib_options) 的選用物件。傳回包含壓縮資料的 Buffer 實例。

``inflateRawSync(`*string*` | `*Buffer*`)``

` `inflateRawSync(` *字串* ` | ` *緩衝區* `)` `

Decompresses a raw stream by using the “deflate” algorithm. Returns Buffer instance that contains the decompressed data.

使用“deflate”演算法解壓縮原始資料流。傳回包含解壓縮資料的 Buffer 實例。

``inflateSync(`*string*` | `*Buffer*`)``

` `inflateSync(` *字串* ` | ` *緩衝區* `)` `

Decompresses a stream by using the “deflate” algorithm. Returns Buffer instance that contains the decompressed data.

使用“deflate”演算法解壓縮資料流。傳回包含解壓縮資料的 Buffer 實例。

#### zlib options｜zlib 選項

-   `chunkSize` — is an integer, by default is `1024`  
    `chunkSize` — 是一個整數，預設值為`1024`
-   `dictionary` — is a `Buffer`, `TypedArray`, or `DataView`. by default is empty  
    `dictionary` — 是`Buffer`, `TypedArray`或`DataView` 。預設為空。
-   `level` — is an integer, compression only, see [zlib\_compression\_levels](#zlib_compression_levels)  
    `level` — 是一個整數，僅用於壓縮，請參閱 [zlib\_compression\_levels](#zlib_compression_levels)
-   `memLevel` — is an integer from `1` to `9`, compression only  
    `memLevel` — 是介於`1`和`9`之間的整數，僅壓縮
-   `strategy` — is an integer, compression only, see [zlib\_compression\_strategy](#zlib_compression_strategy)  
    `strategy` — 是一個整數，僅用於壓縮，請參閱 [zlib\_compression\_strategy](#zlib_compression_strategy)
-   `windowBits` — is an integer from `-15` to `-9` for raw data, from `9` to `15` for an ordinary stream  
    `windowBits` — 對於原始數據，是一個介於`-15`到`-9`之間的整數；對於普通流，是一個介於`9`到`15`之間的整數。

#### zlib compression levels｜zlib 壓縮級別

|   |   |
| --- | --- |
| Name<br>名稱 | Description<br>描述 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_NO_COMPRESSION` | no compression<br>無壓縮 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_BEST_SPEED` | fastest, produces the least compression<br>速度最快，壓縮率最低 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_DEFAULT_COMPRESSION` | trade-off between speed and compression<br>速度與壓縮之間的權衡 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_BEST_COMPRESSION` | slowest, produces the most compression<br>速度最慢，壓縮率最高 |

#### zlib compression strategy｜zlib壓縮策略

|   |   |
| --- | --- |
| Name<br>名稱 | Description<br>描述 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_FILTERED` | Filtered strategy: for the data produced by a filter or predictor<br>過濾策略：針對由過濾器或預測器產生的資料 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_HUFFMAN_ONLY` | Huffman-only strategy: only Huffman encoding, no string matching<br>僅霍夫曼編碼策略：僅進行霍夫曼編碼，不進行字串匹配 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_RLE` | Run Length Encoding strategy: limit match distances to one, better compression of PNG image data<br>遊程編碼策略：將匹配距離限制為 1，更好地壓縮PNG影像資料 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_FIXED` | Fixed table strategy: prevents the use of dynamic Huffman codes, a simpler decoder for special applications<br>固定表策略：避免使用動態霍夫曼碼，為特殊應用提供更簡單的解碼器 |
| `zlib.constants.Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY` | Default strategy, suitable for general purpose compression<br>預設策略，適用於通用壓縮 |
